```rust
/// A branch step with `paths` (label, steps) and `otherwise`.
fn branch(id: &str, on: BranchOn, paths: Vec<(&str, Vec<Step>)>, otherwise: Vec<Step>) -> Step {
    Step {
        id: id.into(),
        name: format!("Branch {id}"),
        kind: StepKind::Branch {
            on,
            paths: paths
                .into_iter()
                .map(|(label, steps)| BranchPath {
                    label: label.into(),
                    steps,
                })
                .collect(),
            otherwise,
        },
    }
}

/// An agent step that may report `outcomes`.
fn reporting(id: &str, name: &str, member: &Dot, outcomes: &[&str]) -> Step {
    let mut step = agent(id, name, member, "Look into it.");
    if let StepKind::Agent { outcomes: o, .. } = &mut step.kind {
        *o = outcomes.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect();
    }
    step
}

/// The step ids of the workflow run's step runs, oldest first.
fn ran(wr: &WorkflowRun) -> Vec<&str> {
    wr.steps.iter().map(|s| s.step_id.as_str()).collect()
}

/// The path the newest run of the branch step `id` chose (`None` for `otherwise`).
fn chose(wr: &WorkflowRun, id: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let run = wr.steps.iter().rev().find(|s| s.step_id == id).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(run.status, StepRunStatus::Succeeded);
    let path = &run.decision.as_ref().unwrap()["path"];
    path.as_str().map(str::to_string)
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_branch_on_project_takes_the_matching_path_then_the_step_after_it() {
    let w = world().await;
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Ship",
            vec![
                branch(
                    "pick",
                    BranchOn::Project,
                    vec![
                        ("Other app", vec![agent("other", "Other", &w.dev, "No.")]),
                        // The project is "Dots": labels match without case.
                        ("dots", vec![agent("dots", "Ship Dots", &w.dev, "Ship it.")]),
                    ],
                    vec![agent("else", "Else", &w.dev, "No.")],
                ),
                agent("after", "After", &w.helper, "Tell the person."),
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    assert_eq!(ran(&wr), ["pick", "dots", "after"]);
    assert_eq!(chose(&wr, "pick").as_deref(), Some("dots"));
    assert_eq!(wr.current_step, None);
    // Only the path taken ran.
    assert_eq!(w.engine.prompts_of("dev").len(), 1);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn no_matching_path_takes_otherwise_and_an_empty_otherwise_goes_straight_on() {
    let w = world().await;
    let web = || vec![("Web", vec![agent("web", "Web", &w.dev, "No.")])];
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Otherwise",
            vec![
                branch(
                    "pick",
                    BranchOn::Project,
                    web(),
                    vec![agent("else", "Else", &w.dev, "Do the rest.")],
                ),
                agent("after", "After", &w.helper, "Tell the person."),
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    assert_eq!(ran(&wr), ["pick", "else", "after"]);
    assert_eq!(chose(&wr, "pick"), None);

    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Straight on",
            vec![
                branch("pick", BranchOn::Project, web(), vec![]),
                agent("after", "After", &w.helper, "Tell the person."),
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    assert_eq!(ran(&wr), ["pick", "after"]);
    assert_eq!(chose(&wr, "pick"), None);

    // A branch that is the last step and takes an empty path ends the run.
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Last",
            vec![branch("pick", BranchOn::Project, web(), vec![])],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    assert_eq!(ran(&wr), ["pick"]);
    assert_eq!(wr.current_step, None);
}

/// The flow of the outcome tests: the dev triages and may report `fixed` or `needs_info`.
async fn triage_flow(w: &World) -> String {
    w.workflow(
        "Triage",
        vec![
            reporting("triage", "Triage", &w.dev, &["fixed", "needs_info"]),
            branch(
                "route",
                BranchOn::Outcome,
                vec![
                    ("fixed", vec![agent("ship", "Ship", &w.helper, "Ship.")]),
                    (
                        "needs_info",
                        vec![agent("ask", "Ask", &w.helper, "Ask the person.")],
                    ),
                ],
                vec![agent("else", "Else", &w.helper, "Look again.")],
            ),
        ],
    )
    .await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_branch_on_outcome_takes_the_path_of_the_outcome_reported() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.engine.set(&w.dev.spec.name, vec![Act::Gate]);
    let flow = triage_flow(&w).await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let step_run = w.until(&wr.id, |wr| !wr.steps.is_empty()).await.steps[0].clone();
    // The agent reports while its step runs.
    w.store
        .set_step_outcome(&step_run.id, Some("needs_info"))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    w.engine.gate.notify_one();
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    assert_eq!(ran(&wr), ["triage", "route", "ask"]);
    assert_eq!(chose(&wr, "route").as_deref(), Some("needs_info"));
    // The hand-off carries the outcome.
    let triage = &wr.steps[0];
    assert_eq!(triage.outcome.as_deref(), Some("needs_info"));
    assert_eq!(
        triage.handoff.as_ref().unwrap().outcome.as_deref(),
        Some("needs_info")
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_branch_on_outcome_with_no_outcome_reported_takes_otherwise() {
    let w = world().await;
    let flow = triage_flow(&w).await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    assert_eq!(ran(&wr), ["triage", "route", "else"]);
    assert_eq!(chose(&wr, "route"), None);
    assert_eq!(wr.steps[0].handoff.as_ref().unwrap().outcome, None);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_branch_inside_a_path_takes_its_own_path_and_the_run_climbs_out_of_both() {
    let w = world().await;
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Nested",
            vec![
                branch(
                    "outer",
                    BranchOn::Project,
                    vec![(
                        "Dots",
                        vec![branch(
                            "inner",
                            BranchOn::Project,
                            vec![("Other app", vec![agent("other", "Other", &w.dev, "No.")])],
                            vec![agent("deep", "Deep", &w.dev, "Work.")],
                        )],
                    )],
                    vec![],
                ),
                agent("last", "Last", &w.helper, "Tell the person."),
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    assert_eq!(ran(&wr), ["outer", "inner", "deep", "last"]);
    assert_eq!(chose(&wr, "outer").as_deref(), Some("Dots"));
    assert_eq!(chose(&wr, "inner"), None);
    // The tree in run order: outer, inner, other, deep, last.
    let deep = &w.engine.prompts_of("dev")[0];
    assert!(deep.contains("- Step: Deep (4 of 5)"), "{deep}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_step_after_a_branch_gets_the_hand_offs_of_the_path_taken() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.helper.spec.name,
        vec![Act::Finish("Task: ship the fix."), Act::Finish("Reviewed.")],
    );
    w.engine.set(
        &w.dev.spec.name,
        vec![Act::Write("fix.txt", "Fixed on Dots.")],
    );
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Review",
            vec![
                agent("task", "Write the task", &w.helper, "Write it."),
                branch(
                    "pick",
                    BranchOn::Project,
                    vec![
                        ("Other app", vec![agent("skip", "Skipped", &w.dev, "No.")]),
                        ("Dots", vec![agent("fix", "Fix Dots", &w.dev, "Fix it.")]),
                    ],
                    vec![],
                ),
                agent("review", "Review", &w.helper, "Review it."),
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    assert_eq!(ran(&wr), ["task", "pick", "fix", "review"]);
    // The step on the path gets the hand-off before the branch, numbered in the whole tree.
    let fix = &w.engine.prompts_of("dev")[0];
    for part in [
        "- Step: Fix Dots (4 of 5)",
        "#### Write the task\n\nTask: ship the fix.",
    ] {
        assert!(fix.contains(part), "missing {part:?} in:\n{fix}");
    }
    // The step after the branch gets both, the path's last, and never the path not taken.
    let review = &w.engine.prompts_of("helper")[1];
    for part in [
        "- Step: Review (5 of 5)",
        "### Earlier steps\n\n#### Write the task\n\nTask: ship the fix.\n\n#### Fix Dots\n\nFixed on Dots.",
    ] {
        assert!(review.contains(part), "missing {part:?} in:\n{review}");
    }
    assert!(!review.contains("Skipped"), "{review}");
    // And starts from the path's branch.
    let fixed = &wr.steps[2].handoff.as_ref().unwrap().branches[0].branch;
    let tip = git_out(&w.repo, &["rev-parse", &format!("refs/heads/{fixed}")]);
    let runs = w.store.runs_of_step_run(&wr.steps[3].id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(runs[0].base_commit.as_deref(), Some(tip.as_str()));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_pass_finishes_a_branch_cut_off_before_it_chose() {
    let w = world().await;
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Cut",
            vec![
                check("check"),
                branch(
                    "pick",
                    BranchOn::Project,
                    vec![("Dots", vec![agent("dots", "Dots", &w.dev, "Work.")])],
                    vec![],
                ),
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, waits_on(WaitingOn::Check)).await;
    // The check was approved and the branch's step run created, but the move stopped there.
    w.store
        .set_step_run(
            &wr.steps[0].id,
            &dots_core::model::StepRunChange {
                status: Some(StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        )
        .await
        .unwrap();
    w.store
        .create_step_run(&wr.id, "pick", 1, StepRunStatus::Running)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    w.store
        .set_workflow_run(
            &wr.id,
            &dots_core::model::WorkflowRunChange {
                status: Some(WorkflowRunStatus::Running),
                waiting_on: Some(None),
                current_step: Some(Some("pick".into())),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        )
        .await
        .unwrap();
    w.workflows.pass().await.unwrap();
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    // The same step run chose: no second attempt.
    assert_eq!(ran(&wr), ["check", "pick", "dots"]);
    assert_eq!(chose(&wr, "pick").as_deref(), Some("Dots"));
}
```

```rust
    /// Records the outcome label the step run's agent reported (`None` clears it). The label
    /// is not checked here: the tool that reports it checks it against the step's outcomes.
    pub async fn set_step_outcome(
        &self,
        step_run_id: &str,
        outcome: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<StepRun> {
        let done = sqlx::query("UPDATE workflow_step_runs SET outcome = ? WHERE id = ?")
            .bind(outcome)
            .bind(step_run_id)
            .execute(&self.pool)
            .await?;
        if done.rows_affected() == 0 {
            return Err(Error::NotFound(format!("step run {step_run_id}")));
        }
        self.get_step_run(step_run_id).await
    }
```

**with:**

```rust

/// The newest succeeded step run of `step_id`.
fn succeeded<'a>(wr: &'a WorkflowRun, step_id: &str) -> Option<&'a StepRun> {
    wr.steps
        .iter()
        .rev()
        .find(|s| s.step_id == step_id && s.status == StepRunStatus::Succeeded)
}

/// The attempt a new step run of `step_id` is: one more than the runs it has had.
fn next_attempt(wr: &WorkflowRun, step_id: &str) -> u32 {
    wr.steps.iter().filter(|s| s.step_id == step_id).count() as u32 + 1
}

/// Whether `id` is `step` or a step inside it, at any depth.
fn holds(step: &Step, id: &str) -> bool {
    step.id == id
        || match &step.kind {
            StepKind::Branch {
                paths, otherwise, ..
            } => paths
                .iter()
                .flat_map(|p| &p.steps)
                .chain(otherwise)
                .any(|s| holds(s, id)),
            _ => false,
        }
}

/// The steps of the path the branch step `branch` took in this run: the path its newest
/// succeeded step run chose (`decision.path`), `otherwise` when it chose none. Empty for a
/// branch that has not run and for the other kinds.
fn taken<'a>(wr: &WorkflowRun, branch: &'a Step) -> &'a [Step] {
    let StepKind::Branch {
        paths, otherwise, ..
    } = &branch.kind
    else {
        return &[];
    };
    let Some(run) = succeeded(wr, &branch.id) else {
        return &[];
    };
    let label = run
        .decision
        .as_ref()
        .and_then(|d| d.get("path"))
        .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str);
    match label {
        Some(label) => paths
            .iter()
            .find(|p| p.label.trim() == label)
            .map_or(&[], |p| p.steps.as_slice()),
        None => otherwise,
    }
}

/// Pushes the steps that ran inside `step` in this run, in run order: for a branch, the steps
/// of the path it took and, recursively, of the branches on that path.
fn ran_inside<'a>(wr: &WorkflowRun, step: &'a Step, out: &mut Vec<&'a Step>) {
    for s in taken(wr, step) {
        out.push(s);
        ran_inside(wr, s, out);
    }
}

/// The steps that ran before the step `id` in this run, oldest first: `Definition::before`,
/// with the steps of the path each earlier branch took after that branch. A path a branch did
/// not take is never in it. The branches `id` is inside are not opened: the steps of its own
/// path before it are already in `before`.
fn ran_before<'a>(wr: &'a WorkflowRun, id: &str) -> Vec<&'a Step> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for step in wr.definition.before(id) {
        out.push(step);
        if !holds(step, id) {
            ran_inside(wr, step, &mut out);
        }
    }
    out
}

/// The step to begin after the step `id` succeeded: for a branch, the first step of the path
/// it took, else (an empty path) the step after the branch; for the others the step after it.
/// `None` past the last step: the workflow run has succeeded.
fn next_of<'a>(wr: &'a WorkflowRun, id: &str) -> Option<&'a Step> {
    let step = wr.definition.step(id)?;
    taken(wr, step)
        .first()
        .or_else(|| wr.definition.next_after(id))
}

/// The path a branch step takes (section 5.6): the first whose label matches, `None` for
/// `otherwise`. On `outcome` the label is compared with the outcome the newest succeeded run
/// of the nearest agent step before it reported, as the definition declares it (after
/// trimming); on `project` with the workflow run's project name, without case.
fn choose<'a>(
    wr: &WorkflowRun,
    id: &str,
    on: BranchOn,
    paths: &'a [BranchPath],
) -> Option<&'a BranchPath> {
    match on {
        BranchOn::Outcome => {
            let source = wr
                .definition
                .before(id)
                .into_iter()
                .rev()
                .find(|s| matches!(s.kind, StepKind::Agent { .. }))?;
            let outcome = succeeded(wr, &source.id)?.outcome.as_deref()?.trim();
            paths.iter().find(|p| p.label.trim() == outcome)
        }
        BranchOn::Project => {
            let project = wr.project.name.trim();
            paths
                .iter()
                .find(|p| p.label.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case(project))
        }
    }
}

/// The hand-offs of the agent steps that ran before the step `id` (`ran_before`), oldest
/// first: each step's newest succeeded run.
fn handoffs_before<'a>(wr: &'a WorkflowRun, id: &str) -> Vec<(&'a Step, &'a Handoff)> {
    ran_before(wr, id)
        .into_iter()
        .filter(|s| matches!(s.kind, StepKind::Agent { .. }))
        .filter_map(|s| Some((s, succeeded(wr, &s.id)?.handoff.as_ref()?)))
        .collect()
```

```rust
    /// Begins `next`; `None` (past the last step) succeeds the workflow run.
    async fn begin_or_finish(&self, wr: &WorkflowRun, next: Option<&Step>) -> Result<()> {
        match next {
            Some(step) => self.begin_step(wr, step).await,
            None => self.finish(wr).await,

                    current_step: Some(None),
```

```rust
    /// Runs the branch step `step` (section 5.6): chooses its path (`choose`) and records the
    /// choice on a step run that succeeds at once, `decision: {"path": <label> | null}`, with
    /// the branch as the current step. `step_run` is one a move cut off left running. Gives the
    /// step to begin next: the path's first, else the step after the branch.
    async fn take_branch<'a>(
        &self,
        wr: &'a WorkflowRun,
        step: &'a Step,
        step_run: Option<&StepRun>,
    ) -> Result<Option<&'a Step>> {
        let StepKind::Branch {
            on,
            paths,
            otherwise,
        } = &step.kind
        else {
            return Ok(wr.definition.next_after(&step.id));
        };
        let path = choose(wr, &step.id, *on, paths);
        let step_run = match step_run {
            Some(run) => run.clone(),
            None => {
                let attempt = next_attempt(wr, &step.id);
                self.store
                    .create_step_run(&wr.id, &step.id, attempt, StepRunStatus::Running)
                    .await?
            }
        };
        self.store
            .set_workflow_run(
                &wr.id,
                &WorkflowRunChange {
                    current_step: Some(Some(step.id.clone())),
                    ..Default::default()
                },
            )
            .await?;
        // One write: the choice and the end, so a branch that reads as succeeded has chosen.
        self.store
            .set_step_run(
                &step_run.id,
                &StepRunChange {
                    status: Some(StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
                    decision: Some(Some(json!({"path": path.map(|p| p.label.trim())}))),
                    ..Default::default()
                },
            )
            .await?;
        let first = path
            .map_or(otherwise.as_slice(), |p| p.steps.as_slice())
            .first();
        Ok(first.or_else(|| wr.definition.next_after(&step.id)))
    }
```

---

### Task 3: Hand-offs are built outside the workflow loop's lock

Dots' commit for a hand-off can take up to 120 s, and plan A held `Workflows::lock` through it. Now `watch_agent` starts a job (`workflows/jobs.rs`) that builds the hand-off unlocked, then takes the lock and writes it only if the step is still open and its runs are unchanged. A Stop meanwhile wins; a job lost to a restart is started again by a later pass. A semaphore gate lets the tests hold a job.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/dots-core/src/workflows/jobs.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dots-core/src/workflows.rs`
- Test: `crates/dots-core/tests/workflows_test.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2's `watch_agent`, `begin_or_finish`, `next_of`.
- Produces: `Workflows` fields `this: Weak<Self>` (`new` uses `Arc::new_cyclic`), `jobs: std::sync::Mutex<HashSet<String>>`, `gate`; in `workflows/jobs.rs`: `pub fn has_job(&self, step_run_id: &str) -> bool`; `#[doc(hidden)] pub fn hold_jobs(&self, gate: Option<Arc<tokio::sync::Semaphore>>)`; `pub(super) fn start_job<F, Fut>(&self, step_run_id: &str, work: F)` (`F: FnOnce(Arc<Self>) -> Fut`, `Fut: Future<Output = Result<()>> + Send + 'static`); `pub(super) async fn still_open(&self, id: &str, step_run_id: &str) -> Result<Option<(WorkflowRun, StepRun)>>`; private `async fn through_gate(&self) -> bool` (Task 8 makes it `pub(super)`); `pub(super) fn start_hand_off(&self, wr: &WorkflowRun, step_run: &StepRun, runs: Vec<Run>)`. Log line `a workflow step's job failed: <e>`.

**with:**

```rust
impl World {
    /// Holds every hand-off job at its gate (`Workflows::hold_jobs`), starts a run of `flow`
    /// and waits until its first step's runs have ended and its hand-off job is held. Gives the
    /// workflow run, the step run's id and the gate.
    async fn held_hand_off(
        &self,
        flow: &str,
    ) -> (WorkflowRun, String, Arc<tokio::sync::Semaphore>) {
        let gate = Arc::new(tokio::sync::Semaphore::new(0));
        self.workflows.hold_jobs(Some(gate.clone()));
        let wr = self.start(flow).await;
        let step = self.until(&wr.id, |wr| !wr.steps.is_empty()).await.steps[0]
            .id
            .clone();
        let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(60);
        while !self.workflows.has_job(&step) {
            assert!(
                std::time::Instant::now() < deadline,
                "no hand-off job started"
            );
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(30)).await;
        }
        (wr, step, gate)
    }

    /// Waits until the step run `step` has no job in flight.
    async fn job_ended(&self, step: &str) {
        let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(60);
        while self.workflows.has_job(step) {
            assert!(
                std::time::Instant::now() < deadline,
                "the hand-off job did not end"
            );
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(30)).await;
        }
    }

    /// A two-step flow whose first step writes a file: its hand-off has a commit to make.
    async fn two_steps(&self) -> String {
        self.engine.set(
            &self.dev.spec.name,
            vec![Act::Write("one.txt", "First."), Act::Finish("Second.")],
        );
        self.workflow(
            "Two",
            vec![
                agent("one", "First", &self.dev, "Start."),
                agent("two", "Second", &self.dev, "Go on."),
            ],
        )
        .await
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_hand_off_being_built_holds_up_neither_the_loop_nor_a_stop() {
    let w = world().await;
    let flow = w.two_steps().await;
    let (held, step, _gate) = w.held_hand_off(&flow).await;
    let wr = w.store.get_workflow_run(&held.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(wr.steps[0].status, StepRunStatus::Running);
    assert!(wr.steps[0].handoff.is_none());
    // Another workflow run moves meanwhile: the loop begins its check, and the person's
    // approval ends it.
    let checked = w.workflow("Check", vec![check("check")]).await;
    let other = w.start(&checked).await;
    let other = w.until(&other.id, waits_on(WaitingOn::Check)).await;
    let done = tokio::time::timeout(
        Duration::from_secs(10),
        w.workflows.approve_check(&other.id, None),
    )
    .await
    .expect("the approval waited on the job")
    .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(done.status, WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded);
    // Stopping the run whose hand-off is held does not wait for it.
    let stopped = tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(10), w.workflows.stop(&held.id))
        .await
        .expect("the stop waited on the job")
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(stopped.status, WorkflowRunStatus::Cancelled);
    assert!(w.workflows.has_job(&step));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_stop_during_a_hand_off_wins() {
    let w = world().await;
    let flow = w.two_steps().await;
    let (held, step, gate) = w.held_hand_off(&flow).await;
    w.workflows.stop(&held.id).await.unwrap();
    // The job goes on to build the hand-off, then finds the step cancelled.
    gate.add_permits(1);
    w.job_ended(&step).await;
    w.workflows.pass().await.unwrap();
    let wr = w.store.get_workflow_run(&held.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(wr.status, WorkflowRunStatus::Cancelled);
    assert_eq!(ran(&wr), ["one"]);
    assert_eq!(wr.steps[0].status, StepRunStatus::Cancelled);
    assert!(wr.steps[0].handoff.is_none());
    assert_eq!(w.engine.prompts_of("dev").len(), 1);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_lost_hand_off_is_built_again_by_a_later_pass() {
    let w = world().await;
    let flow = w.two_steps().await;
    let (held, step, gate) = w.held_hand_off(&flow).await;
    // The job is dropped before it writes, as Dots stopping there would drop it; the jobs
    // after it are let through.
    w.workflows.hold_jobs(None);
    gate.close();
    w.job_ended(&step).await;
    w.workflows.pass().await.unwrap();
    let wr = w
        .until(&held.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    assert_eq!(ran(&wr), ["one", "two"]);
    let first = &wr.steps[0].handoff.as_ref().unwrap().branches[0].branch;
    assert_eq!(
        git_out(&w.repo, &["show", &format!("{first}:one.txt")]),
        "First."
    );
    // One attempt of each step: the step run lost its job, not its place.
    assert!(wr.steps.iter().all(|s| s.attempt == 1));
}
```

**Create `crates/dots-core/src/workflows/jobs.rs`:**

```rust
//! Jobs: the slow work of a step done outside `Workflows::lock` (teams plan B, "Off-lock
//! work"), so a pass, a Stop or the person's actions never wait on it. Building an agent step's
//! hand-off runs Dots' commit, up to two minutes; a deploy pushes and calls gh.
//!
//! A job is keyed by its step run. A pass starts it when it finds the step's work ended and no
//! job of that step run in flight. The job does its slow part unlocked, then takes the lock,
//! reads the workflow run and the step run again and writes its result only if the step run is
//! still the open current step (a Stop meanwhile wins), moves the workflow run on and wakes the
//! loop. It leaves the set of jobs in flight however it ends. A job lost on the way (Dots
//! stopped) leaves its step run open with no job, which a later pass finds: a hand-off is
//! built again (Dots' commit is safe to make twice); a step whose slow part must not run twice
//! without the person (a deploy) is failed instead.

use std::future::Future;
use std::sync::Arc;

use tokio::sync::Semaphore;

use super::{next_of, Workflows};
use crate::model::{
    Handoff, Run, StepRun, StepRunChange, StepRunStatus, WorkflowRun, WorkflowRunStatus,
};
use crate::{Error, Result};

/// Takes a job out of the set of jobs in flight when it ends, however it ends (an error, a
/// panic, a lost gate).
struct InFlight {
    workflows: Arc<Workflows>,
    step_run_id: String,
}

impl Drop for InFlight {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.workflows
            .jobs
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner())
            .remove(&self.step_run_id);
    }
}

impl Workflows {
    /// Whether a job of the step run `step_run_id` is in flight.
    pub fn has_job(&self, step_run_id: &str) -> bool {
        self.jobs
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner())
            .contains(step_run_id)
    }

    /// For tests: makes every job wait on `gate` before its slow part, one permit per job
    /// (`None` lets them through). Closing the gate drops the jobs waiting on it, and the ones
    /// that reach it, without a write, as Dots stopping there would. A test holds a job in its
    /// slow part this way, with no hook in the work itself.
    #[doc(hidden)]
    pub fn hold_jobs(&self, gate: Option<Arc<Semaphore>>) {
        *self.gate.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()) = gate;
    }

    /// Starts `work` as the job of the step run `step_run_id`, unless one is in flight. `work`
    /// gets the workflows to act on; it ends `Ok` when it has moved the workflow run on or
    /// found nothing to do, which wakes the loop. An error is logged and wakes nothing: the
    /// next pass (an event, the tick) starts the job again.
    pub(super) fn start_job<F, Fut>(&self, step_run_id: &str, work: F)
    where
        F: FnOnce(Arc<Self>) -> Fut,
        Fut: Future<Output = Result<()>> + Send + 'static,
    {
        // Gone only while the workflows are dropped: nothing would read the result.
        let Some(this) = self.this.upgrade() else {
            return;
        };
        if !self
            .jobs
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner())
            .insert(step_run_id.to_string())
        {
            return;
        }
        let in_flight = InFlight {
            workflows: this.clone(),
            step_run_id: step_run_id.to_string(),
        };
        let job = work(this.clone());
        let id = step_run_id.to_string();
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let result = job.await;
            // Out of the set before the wake, so the pass it makes may start the job again.
            drop(in_flight);
            match result {
                Ok(()) => this.wake(),
                Err(e) => tracing::warn!(step_run = %id, "a workflow step's job failed: {e}"),
            }
        });
    }

    /// Waits on the test gate, if one is set. `false`: the gate was closed, and the job ends
    /// here without a write.
    async fn through_gate(&self) -> bool {
        let gate = self.gate.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).clone();
        match gate {
            Some(gate) => match gate.acquire().await {
                Ok(permit) => {
                    permit.forget();
                    true
                }
                Err(_) => false,
            },
            None => true,
        }
    }

    /// The workflow run `id` and its step run `step_run_id`, read under the lock by a job about
    /// to write: `None` unless the workflow run is running with the step run as its current
    /// step and the step run is still `Running` (stopped, failed or moved on meanwhile, the job
    /// writes nothing).
    pub(super) async fn still_open(
        &self,
        id: &str,
        step_run_id: &str,
    ) -> Result<Option<(WorkflowRun, StepRun)>> {
        let wr = self.store.get_workflow_run(id).await?;
        let Some(step_run) = wr.steps.iter().find(|s| s.id == step_run_id).cloned() else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let open = wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Running
            && wr.current_step.as_deref() == Some(step_run.step_id.as_str())
            && step_run.status == StepRunStatus::Running;
        Ok(open.then_some((wr, step_run)))
    }

    /// Starts the job that ends the agent step run `step_run`, whose runs `runs` have all
    /// ended with the newest succeeded: it builds the step's hand-off (`handoff_of`, Dots'
    /// commit included), then succeeds the step run with it and begins the step after it.
    pub(super) fn start_hand_off(&self, wr: &WorkflowRun, step_run: &StepRun, runs: Vec<Run>) {
        let wr = wr.clone();
        let step_run_id = step_run.id.clone();
        self.start_job(&step_run.id, move |this| async move {
            this.hand_off(wr, step_run_id, runs).await
        });
    }

    /// The hand-off job (`start_hand_off`).
    async fn hand_off(&self, wr: WorkflowRun, step_run_id: String, runs: Vec<Run>) -> Result<()> {
        if !self.through_gate().await {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let built = self.handoff_of(&wr, &runs).await;
        let _one = self.lock.lock().await;
        let Some((wr, step_run)) = self.still_open(&wr.id, &step_run_id).await? else {
            return Ok(());
        };
        // A run that joined the thread meanwhile (a follow-up, the person's message) has work
        // this hand-off lacks: the pass that sees it end starts the job again.
        let now = self.store.runs_of_step_run(&step_run_id).await?;
        let same = now.len() == runs.len()
            && now
                .iter()
                .zip(&runs)
                .all(|(a, b)| a.id == b.id && a.status == b.status);
        if !same {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let Some(step) = wr.definition.step(&step_run.step_id) else {
            return Err(Error::Other(format!(
                "step {} is not in the workflow",
                step_run.step_id
            )));
        };
        // The step run read under the lock carries the outcome its runs reported.
        let handoff = Handoff {
            outcome: step_run.outcome.clone(),
            ..built
        };
        self.store
            .set_step_run(
                &step_run.id,
                &StepRunChange {
                    status: Some(StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
                    handoff: Some(Some(handoff)),
                    ..Default::default()
                },
            )
            .await?;
        self.step_ended(&wr, &step.id);
        let wr = self.store.get_workflow_run(&wr.id).await?;
        self.begin_or_finish(&wr, next_of(&wr, &step.id)).await
    }
}
```

### Task 4: Agent steps report outcomes and ask for send-backs with workflow tools

`team_tools.rs` gains `start_member`, `report_outcome` and `send_back` under the policy name `Workflow`. A run of an open agent step gets `StepTools` (its outcomes and send-back targets) through `RunTools`, and every offer point (Claude's `--allowedTools`, Dots' MCP server, the local engine) asks `RunTools::offers`. `report_outcome` writes the step run's `outcome`; `send_back` records `{"to", "note"}` on the step run (Task 6 performs it). The `## Workflow` section lists the tools under `### Workflow tools`, by the names the member's model knows. `start_member` is defined but not offered until Task 5.

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_step_run_records_the_send_back_it_asked_for() {
    let f = fixture().await;
    let wr = f.store.create_workflow_run(&f.new).await.unwrap();
    let step = f
        .store
        .create_step_run(&wr.id, "a", 1, StepRunStatus::Running)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(step.send_back, None);
    let asked = json!({"to": "a", "note": "Use the light icon."});
    let recorded = f
        .store
        .set_step_send_back(&step.id, Some(&asked))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(recorded.send_back, Some(asked.clone()));
    // The workflow run reads it with its steps, and a change that does not name it keeps it.
    let done = f
        .store
        .set_step_run(
            &step.id,
            &StepRunChange {
                status: Some(StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        )
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(done.send_back, Some(asked.clone()));
    let read = f.store.get_workflow_run(&wr.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(read.steps[0].send_back, Some(asked));
    let cleared = f.store.set_step_send_back(&step.id, None).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(cleared.send_back, None);
    assert!(matches!(
        f.store.set_step_send_back("nope", None).await,
        Err(Error::NotFound(_))
    ));
}
```

```rust
/// An agent step of `member` with the outcomes it may report and the steps it may send back to.
fn agent_step(id: &str, name: &str, member: &Dot, outcomes: &[&str], back: &[&str]) -> Step {
    Step {
        id: id.into(),
        name: name.into(),
        kind: StepKind::Agent {
            member: member.id.clone(),
            job: format!("{name}."),
            can_send_back_to: back.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect(),
            outcomes: outcomes.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect(),
            delegate: None,
        },
    }
}

/// A team of `members` with the project "Dots" and a manual workflow "Steps" of `steps`;
/// starts a run of it.
async fn run_steps(t: &Rt, members: &[&Dot], steps: Vec<Step>) -> WorkflowRun {
    let team =
        t.rt.teams()
            .create(&TeamSpec {
                org_id: None,
                workspace_id: "default".into(),
                name: "Dots management".into(),
                description: String::new(),
                members: members
                    .iter()
                    .map(|d| MemberSpec {
                        dot_id: d.id.clone(),
                        role: format!("{} agent", d.spec.name),
                    })
                    .collect(),
            })
            .await
            .unwrap();
    let repo = t.dir.path().join("repo");
    init_repo(&repo);
    t.rt.teams()
        .add_project(
            &team.id,
            &ProjectSpec {
                name: "Dots".into(),
                repo_path: repo.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
                default_branch: None,
                remote: "origin".into(),
            },
        )
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let flow =
        t.rt.teams()
            .create_workflow(
                &team.id,
                &WorkflowSpec {
                    name: "Steps".into(),
                    definition: Definition {
                        trigger: Trigger::Manual,
                        steps,
                        budget: Budget::default(),
                    },
                    enabled: true,
                },
            )
            .await
            .unwrap();
    let project =
        t.rt.store()
            .get_team_project_id(&flow.id, "Dots")
            .await
            .unwrap();
    t.rt.workflows()
        .start(dots_core::workflows::StartRequest {
            workflow_id: flow.id,
            project_id: project,
            task: "Fix the icon.".into(),
            trigger: WorkflowTrigger::Manual,
            origin_run_id: None,
            test: false,
        })
        .await
        .unwrap()
}

/// The workflow run once `done` holds of it, within 30 s.
async fn until(t: &Rt, id: &str, done: impl Fn(&WorkflowRun) -> bool) -> WorkflowRun {
    let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(30);
    loop {
        let wr = t.rt.workflows().get(id).await.unwrap();
        if done(&wr) {
            return wr;
        }
        assert!(std::time::Instant::now() < deadline, "{wr:#?}");
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
    }
}

const WORKFLOW_TOOLS: [&str; 4] = [
    "start_workflow",
    "start_member",
    "report_outcome",
    "send_back",
];

/// The workflow tools among the request's tools.
fn workflow_tools(req: &Value) -> Vec<String> {
    tool_names(req)
        .into_iter()
        .filter(|n| WORKFLOW_TOOLS.contains(&n.as_str()))
        .collect()
}

/// The local engine: a step with outcomes reports one, the run that carries the person's answer
/// to its question (a later run of its thread) still has the tool, the last report counts, and
/// the branch after it takes that outcome's path. A step without outcomes or send-backs has no
/// workflow tool.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_local_step_reports_its_outcome_and_the_branch_after_it_takes_that_path() {
    let t = start(json!([])).await;
    let llm = Llm::start(vec![
        text("Built."),
        call("c1", "ask", json!({"question": "Light or dark icon?"})),
        text("Asked."),
        call("c2", "report_outcome", json!({"outcome": "maybe"})),
        call("c3", "report_outcome", json!({"outcome": "approved"})),
        call("c4", "report_outcome", json!({"outcome": " Changes "})),
        text("Needs changes."),
        text("Fixed."),
    ])
    .await;
    let helper =
        t.rt.create_dot(local_spec(&t, &llm.endpoint(), "helper"), None, None)
            .await
            .unwrap();
    let route = Step {
        id: "route".into(),
        name: "Route".into(),
        kind: StepKind::Branch {
            on: BranchOn::Outcome,
            paths: vec![
                BranchPath {
                    label: "approved".into(),
                    steps: vec![Step {
                        id: "check".into(),
                        name: "Your check".into(),
                        kind: StepKind::Check { send_back_to: None },
                    }],
                },
                BranchPath {
                    label: "changes".into(),
                    steps: vec![agent_step("fix", "Fix it", &helper, &[], &[])],
                },
            ],
            otherwise: vec![],
        },
    };
    let wr = run_steps(
        &t,
        &[&helper],
        vec![
            agent_step("build", "Build it", &helper, &[], &[]),
            agent_step(
                "review",
                "Review it",
                &helper,
                &["approved", "changes"],
                &[],
            ),
            route,
        ],
    )
    .await;
    let question = loop {
        let open =
            t.rt.store()
                .list_messages(Some(&helper.id))
                .await
                .unwrap()
                .into_iter()
                .find(|m| m.answered_at.is_none());
        if let Some(q) = open {
            break q;
        }
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(30)).await;
    };
    wait_status(
        t.rt.store(),
        question.run_id.as_deref().unwrap(),
        RunStatus::Succeeded,
        30,
    )
    .await;
    let answer = t.rt.reply(&question.id, "The light one.").await.unwrap();
    let wr = until(&t, &wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded).await;
    let reqs = llm.requests();
    // The build step has no workflow tool; the review step only `report_outcome`, in its first
    // run and in the run that carries the answer.
    assert!(
        workflow_tools(&reqs[0]).is_empty(),
        "{:?}",
        tool_names(&reqs[0])
    );
    assert!(!prompt(&reqs[0]).contains("### Workflow tools"));
    assert_eq!(workflow_tools(&reqs[1]), ["report_outcome"]);
    assert_eq!(workflow_tools(&reqs[3]), ["report_outcome"]);
    let review = prompt(&reqs[1]);
    assert!(
        review.contains(
            "### Workflow tools\n\n- Report how the step came out with `report_outcome`, one of: \"approved\", \"changes\". The workflow goes on by the outcome you report.\n\nWhen you are done"
        ),
        "{review}"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        tool_result(&reqs[4], "c2"),
        "\"maybe\" is not an outcome of this step; use one of: \"approved\", \"changes\"."
    );
    assert_eq!(tool_result(&reqs[5], "c3"), "Outcome recorded.");
    assert_eq!(tool_result(&reqs[6], "c4"), "Outcome recorded.");
    // The last report counts, as the step declares it; the branch took its path.
    let ran: Vec<&str> = wr.steps.iter().map(|s| s.step_id.as_str()).collect();
    assert_eq!(ran, ["build", "review", "route", "fix"]);
    assert_eq!(wr.steps[1].outcome.as_deref(), Some("changes"));
    assert_eq!(
        wr.steps[1].handoff.as_ref().unwrap().outcome.as_deref(),
        Some("changes")
    );
    assert_eq!(wr.steps[2].decision, Some(json!({"path": "changes"})));
    assert_eq!(
        answer.workflow_step_run_id.as_deref(),
        Some(wr.steps[1].id.as_str())
    );
    t.rt.shutdown();
}

/// The local engine: `send_back` names an earlier step by name without case and needs a note;
/// the last call is recorded on the step run. Once the step has ended, its runs have no
/// workflow tools, and a call made with the tools a run had records nothing.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_local_step_asks_for_a_send_back_and_a_closed_step_records_nothing() {
    let t = start(json!([])).await;
    let llm = Llm::start(vec![
        text("Built."),
        call(
            "c1",
            "send_back",
            json!({"to_step": "Nowhere", "note": "x"}),
        ),
        call(
            "c2",
            "send_back",
            json!({"to_step": "build it", "note": "  "}),
        ),
        call(
            "c3",
            "send_back",
            json!({"to_step": "Build it", "note": "First try."}),
        ),
        call(
            "c4",
            "send_back",
            json!({"to_step": "BUILD IT", "note": " Use the light icon. ", "extra": 1}),
        ),
        text("Sent back."),
    ])
    .await;
    let helper =
        t.rt.create_dot(local_spec(&t, &llm.endpoint(), "helper"), None, None)
            .await
            .unwrap();
    let wr = run_steps(
        &t,
        &[&helper],
        vec![
            agent_step("build", "Build it", &helper, &[], &[]),
            agent_step("review", "Review it", &helper, &[], &["build"]),
        ],
    )
    .await;
    let wr = until(&t, &wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded).await;
    let reqs = llm.requests();
    assert_eq!(workflow_tools(&reqs[1]), ["send_back"]);
    let review = prompt(&reqs[1]);
    assert!(
        review.contains(
            "### Workflow tools\n\n- If the work must be redone in an earlier step, send it back with `send_back` to one of: \"Build it\", with a note that says what to change.\n"
        ),
        "{review}"
    );
    let answers: Vec<String> = (1..=4)
        .map(|i| tool_result(&reqs[i + 1], &format!("c{i}")))
        .collect();
    let sent = "Sent back to \"Build it\". End your turn now: the work goes back when you finish.";
    assert_eq!(
        answers,
        [
            "You can send work back only to: \"Build it\".",
            "Give a note of 1 to 8,000 characters that says what to change.",
            sent,
            sent,
        ]
    );
    let step_run = &wr.steps[1];
    assert_eq!(
        step_run.send_back,
        Some(json!({"to": "build", "note": "Use the light icon."}))
    );
    assert_eq!(step_run.outcome, None);
    // The step has ended: a run of its thread has no workflow tools, and a call made with
    // those its run had is refused.
    let run =
        t.rt.store()
            .runs_of_step_run(&step_run.id)
            .await
            .unwrap()
            .remove(0);
    assert_eq!(team_tools::for_run(t.rt.store(), &run).await.unwrap(), None);
    let step = wr.definition.step("review").unwrap();
    let tools = StepTools::of(&step_run.id, step, &wr.definition).unwrap();
    let refused =
        t.rt.workflows()
            .call_send_back(
                &run.id,
                &tools,
                &json!({"to_step": "Build it", "note": "Again."}),
            )
            .await;
    assert_eq!(refused, Err(STEP_CLOSED.to_string()));
    let refused =
        t.rt.workflows()
            .call_report_outcome(&run.id, &tools, &json!({"outcome": "x"}))
            .await;
    assert_eq!(refused, Err(STEP_CLOSED.to_string()));
    let after = t.rt.store().get_step_run(&step_run.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(after.send_back, step_run.send_back);
    assert_eq!(after.outcome, None);
    t.rt.shutdown();
}

/// The Claude engine: Dots' own server lists a step's tools and performs them, the CLI may call
/// them without asking (Dots checks each call itself), and the prompt names them as the CLI's
/// model knows them.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_claude_step_reports_and_sends_back_through_dots_own_server() {
    let t = start(json!([
        {"emit": {"type": "system", "subtype": "init", "session_id": "$SESSION"}},
        {"mcp_list": "dots"},
        {"mcp_call": {"server": "dots", "tool": "report_outcome",
                      "arguments": {"outcome": "approved"}}},
        {"mcp_call": {"server": "dots", "tool": "send_back",
                      "arguments": {"to_step": "build it", "note": "Again."}}},
        {"emit": {"type": "result", "subtype": "success", "is_error": false, "result": "done",
                  "usage": {"input_tokens": 1, "output_tokens": 1}}}
    ]))
    .await;
    let llm = Llm::start(vec![text("Built.")]).await;
    let builder =
        t.rt.create_dot(local_spec(&t, &llm.endpoint(), "builder"), None, None)
            .await
            .unwrap();
    let mut s = spec("reviewer");
    s.workdir = t.dir.path().to_string_lossy().to_string();
    s.workspace_mode = WorkspaceMode::Folder;
    s.policy = Policy::preset(Preset::Trusted);
    let reviewer = t.rt.create_dot(s, None, None).await.unwrap();
    let wr = run_steps(
        &t,
        &[&builder, &reviewer],
        vec![
            agent_step("build", "Build it", &builder, &[], &[]),
            agent_step("review", "Review it", &reviewer, &["approved"], &["build"]),
        ],
    )
    .await;
    let wr = until(&t, &wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded).await;
    let recorded: Value =
        serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(&t.args_out).unwrap()).unwrap();
    let args: Vec<String> = serde_json::from_value(recorded["args"].clone()).unwrap();
    let allowed: Vec<&str> = args
        .iter()
        .skip_while(|a| *a != "--allowedTools")
        .skip(1)
        .take_while(|a| !a.starts_with("--"))
        .map(String::as_str)
        .filter(|a| {
            WORKFLOW_TOOLS
                .iter()
                .any(|t| *a == format!("mcp__dots__{t}"))
        })
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(
        allowed,
        ["mcp__dots__report_outcome", "mcp__dots__send_back"],
        "{args:?}"
    );
    let prompt = recorded["prompt"].as_str().unwrap();
    assert!(
        prompt.contains("with `mcp__dots__report_outcome`, one of: \"approved\".")
            && prompt.contains("send it back with `mcp__dots__send_back` to one of: \"Build it\","),
        "{prompt}"
    );
    let step_run = &wr.steps[1];
    let run =
        t.rt.store()
            .runs_of_step_run(&step_run.id)
            .await
            .unwrap()
            .remove(0);
    let events = t.rt.store().list_events(&run.id, 0).await.unwrap();
    let listed = events.iter().find(|e| e.kind == "raw").unwrap();
    let listed: Value = serde_json::from_str(listed.data["line"].as_str().unwrap()).unwrap();
    let listed: Vec<&str> = listed["tools"]
        .as_array()
        .unwrap()
        .iter()
        .filter_map(Value::as_str)
        .filter(|n| WORKFLOW_TOOLS.contains(n))
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(listed, ["report_outcome", "send_back"]);
    let results: Vec<(&str, bool)> = events
        .iter()
        .filter(|e| e.kind == "tool_result")
        .map(|e| {
            (
                e.data["output"].as_str().unwrap(),
                e.data["is_error"].as_bool().unwrap(),
            )
        })
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(
        results,
        [
            ("Outcome recorded.", false),
            (
                "Sent back to \"Build it\". End your turn now: the work goes back when you finish.",
                false
            ),
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(step_run.outcome.as_deref(), Some("approved"));
    assert_eq!(
        step_run.send_back,
        Some(json!({"to": "build", "note": "Again."}))
    );
    t.rt.shutdown();
}

/// The plain Sandboxed preset asks before a step's run reports its outcome (policy
/// `Workflow`); nothing is recorded until the person allows it.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_sandboxed_step_asks_before_it_reports_an_outcome() {
    let t = start(json!([])).await;
    let llm = Llm::start(vec![
        call("c1", "report_outcome", json!({"outcome": "done"})),
        text("Reported."),
    ])
    .await;
    let mut s = local_spec(&t, &llm.endpoint(), "helper");
    s.policy = Policy::preset(Preset::Sandboxed);
    s.approval_wait_secs = 30;
    let helper = t.rt.create_dot(s, None, None).await.unwrap();
    let mut events = t.rt.subscribe();
    let wr = run_steps(
        &t,
        &[&helper],
        vec![agent_step("review", "Review it", &helper, &["done"], &[])],
    )
    .await;
    let approval = loop {
        if let dots_core::events::RuntimeEvent::ApprovalRequested { approval } =
            events.recv().await.unwrap()
        {
            break approval;
        }
    };
    assert_eq!(approval.tool, "Workflow");
    assert_eq!(approval.input, json!({"outcome": "done"}));
    let held = t.rt.workflows().get(&wr.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(held.steps[0].outcome, None);
    t.rt.decide_approval(&approval.id, true, None, None)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let wr = until(&t, &wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded).await;
    assert_eq!(wr.steps[0].outcome.as_deref(), Some("done"));
    assert_eq!(tool_result(&llm.requests()[1], "c1"), "Outcome recorded.");
    t.rt.shutdown();
}

/// A step of a dot whose policy denies `Workflow` outright has neither the tools nor their
/// lines in its prompt.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_step_of_a_dot_whose_policy_denies_workflow_has_no_workflow_tools() {
    let t = start(json!([])).await;
    let llm = Llm::start(vec![text("Done.")]).await;
    let mut s = local_spec(&t, &llm.endpoint(), "helper");
    s.policy.rules.push(Rule::new("Workflow", Action::Deny));
    let helper = t.rt.create_dot(s, None, None).await.unwrap();
    let wr = run_steps(
        &t,
        &[&helper],
        vec![agent_step("review", "Review it", &helper, &["done"], &[])],
    )
    .await;
    until(&t, &wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded).await;
    let first = llm.requests()[0].clone();
    assert!(
        workflow_tools(&first).is_empty(),
        "{:?}",
        tool_names(&first)
    );
    assert!(!prompt(&first).contains("### Workflow tools"));
    t.rt.shutdown();
}
```

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_step_s_runs_get_its_workflow_tools_and_its_prompt_names_them() {
    let w = world().await;
    let mut review = reporting("review", "Review it", &w.dev, &["fixed", "needs_info"]);
    if let StepKind::Agent {
        can_send_back_to, ..
    } = &mut review.kind
    {
        *can_send_back_to = vec!["write".into()];
    }
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Review",
            vec![
                agent("write", "Write the task", &w.helper, "Write it."),
                review,
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    // A step that declares neither outcomes nor send-backs offers no tool, and its prompt names
    // none.
    let write = w.engine.step_tools_of("helper").remove(0).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(write.step_run_id, wr.steps[0].id);
    assert!(Tool::ALL.iter().all(|t| !write.offers(*t)));
    assert!(!w.engine.prompts_of("helper")[0].contains("### Workflow tools"));
    // The review step's run gets its step run, its outcomes and its targets by name.
    assert_eq!(
        w.engine.step_tools_of("dev"),
        [Some(StepTools {
            step_run_id: wr.steps[1].id.clone(),
            step_id: "review".into(),
            outcomes: vec!["fixed".into(), "needs_info".into()],
            send_back_to: vec![SendBackTarget {
                step_id: "write".into(),
                name: "Write the task".into(),
            }],
        })]
    );
    let prompt = &w.engine.prompts_of("dev")[0];
    assert!(
        prompt.contains(
            "### Workflow tools\n\n- Report how the step came out with `mcp__dots__report_outcome`, one of: \"fixed\", \"needs_info\". The workflow goes on by the outcome you report.\n- If the work must be redone in an earlier step, send it back with `mcp__dots__send_back` to one of: \"Write the task\", with a note that says what to change.\n\nWhen you are done, end with a summary"
        ),
        "{prompt}"
    );
}
```

```rust
    }
```

```rust
    /// Records the send-back an agent step asked for (`{"to": <step id>, "note": ...}`), or
    /// clears it, unchecked: the tool checks it. `Error::NotFound` for an unknown step run.
    pub async fn set_step_send_back(
        &self,
        step_run_id: &str,
        send_back: Option<&Value>,
    ) -> Result<StepRun> {
        let done = sqlx::query("UPDATE workflow_step_runs SET send_back = ? WHERE id = ?")
            .bind(send_back.map(Value::to_string))
            .bind(step_run_id)
            .execute(&self.pool)
            .await?;
        if done.rows_affected() == 0 {
            return Err(Error::NotFound(format!("step run {step_run_id}")));
        }
        self.get_step_run(step_run_id).await
    }
```

```rust
    /// The tool a `Workflow` call with `input` is for, by its arguments.

```

**with:**

```rust

    /// The tool a `Workflow` call with `input` is for, by its arguments: each of the others has
    /// one that `start_workflow` lacks.
    pub fn for_input(input: &Value) -> Tool {
        if input.get("outcome").is_some() {
            Tool::ReportOutcome
        } else if input.get("to_step").is_some() {
            Tool::SendBack
        } else if input.get("member").is_some() {
            Tool::StartMember
        } else {
            Tool::StartWorkflow
        }
    }
```

```rust
/// An earlier step a step may send its work back to.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct SendBackTarget {
    pub step_id: String,
    pub name: String,
}

/// The workflow tools of a run that works for an open agent step (section 7): what the step
/// declares, read from the workflow run's definition when the run starts.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct StepTools {
    pub step_run_id: String,
    pub step_id: String,
    /// The outcome labels it may report, trimmed, in the definition's order.
    pub outcomes: Vec<String>,
    /// The steps it may send its work back to, in the definition's order.
    pub send_back_to: Vec<SendBackTarget>,
}

impl StepTools {
    /// The tools of the step run `step_run_id` of `step`, an agent step of `definition`; `None`
    /// for a step of another kind.
    pub fn of(step_run_id: &str, step: &Step, definition: &Definition) -> Option<StepTools> {
        let StepKind::Agent {
            can_send_back_to,
            outcomes,
            ..
        } = &step.kind
        else {
            return None;
        };
        Some(StepTools {
            step_run_id: step_run_id.to_string(),
            step_id: step.id.clone(),
            outcomes: outcomes.iter().map(|o| o.trim().to_string()).collect(),
            // Validation keeps only earlier agent steps here; a definition that names a step it
            // no longer has gives that step no target.
            send_back_to: can_send_back_to
                .iter()
                .filter_map(|id| definition.step(id))
                .map(|s| SendBackTarget {
                    step_id: s.id.clone(),
                    name: s.name.clone(),
                })
                .collect(),
        })
    }

    /// Whether the run has `tool`: `report_outcome` with outcomes to report, `send_back` with
    /// steps to send back to. `start_member` is not offered yet, nor is `start_workflow`, which
    /// is a chat's tool.
    pub fn offers(&self, tool: Tool) -> bool {
        match tool {
            Tool::ReportOutcome => !self.outcomes.is_empty(),
            Tool::SendBack => !self.send_back_to.is_empty(),
            Tool::StartWorkflow | Tool::StartMember => false,
        }
    }

    /// The lines of the `## Workflow` section that name the run's tools as its model knows
    /// them, with the outcomes and the steps it may send back to; empty without any.
    pub fn prompt_lines(&self, engine: EngineKind) -> String {
        let mut out = String::new();
        if self.offers(Tool::ReportOutcome) {
            out.push_str(&format!(
                "- Report how the step came out with `{}`, one of: {}. The workflow goes on by \
                 the outcome you report.\n",
                Tool::ReportOutcome.model_name(engine),
                quoted(self.outcomes.iter().map(String::as_str))
            ));
        }
        if self.offers(Tool::SendBack) {
            out.push_str(&format!(
                "- If the work must be redone in an earlier step, send it back with `{}` to one \
                 of: {}, with a note that says what to change.\n",
                Tool::SendBack.model_name(engine),
                quoted(self.send_back_to.iter().map(|t| t.name.as_str()))
            ));
        }
        out
    }
}

/// `names` in double quotes, separated by commas: `"a", "b"`.
pub fn quoted<'a>(names: impl Iterator<Item = &'a str>) -> String {
    names
        .map(|n| format!("\"{n}\""))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join(", ")
}

/// The workflow tools of `run`: those of the agent step it works for while that step is open
/// (its step run `running` or `waiting`). A run in a member's thread gets none (task 5's rule:
/// a run whose `root_run_id` has a `workflow_step_members` row); nor does any other run.
pub async fn for_run(store: &Store, run: &Run) -> Result<Option<StepTools>> {
    let Some(step_run_id) = &run.workflow_step_run_id else {
        return Ok(None);
    };
    let step_run = store.get_step_run(step_run_id).await?;
    if !matches!(
        step_run.status,
        StepRunStatus::Running | StepRunStatus::Waiting
    ) {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let wr = store.get_workflow_run(&step_run.workflow_run_id).await?;
    Ok(wr
        .definition
        .step(&step_run.step_id)
        .and_then(|step| StepTools::of(&step_run.id, step, &wr.definition)))
}
```

**Create `crates/dots-core/src/workflows/tools.rs`:**

```rust
//! The workflow tools of an agent step's run (teams spec section 7), as `Workflows` performs
//! them: `report_outcome` records the step's outcome, `send_back` the send-back it asks for.
//! Each call is made under the loop's lock, so it cannot cross a step that ends: a call whose
//! step has closed records nothing. The last call of each wins.

use serde_json::{json, Value};

use super::{Workflows, MAX_TASK};
use crate::model::StepRunStatus;
use crate::team_tools::{quoted, StepTools};

/// Why a call of a step's tool records nothing once the step has ended (a Stop meanwhile).
pub const STEP_CLOSED: &str = "This step of the workflow has ended, so nothing was recorded.";

/// The trimmed text of the argument `key` of `input`; empty when it is missing.
fn arg(input: &Value, key: &str) -> String {
    input
        .get(key)
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        .map(str::trim)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .to_string()
}

/// The declared outcome `given` names: the one equal to it, else the only one equal to it
/// without case. A branch matches the declared label exactly, so that is what is stored.
fn outcome_of<'a>(outcomes: &'a [String], given: &str) -> Option<&'a str> {
    if let Some(o) = outcomes.iter().find(|o| *o == given) {
        return Some(o);
    }
    match outcomes
        .iter()
        .filter(|o| o.eq_ignore_ascii_case(given))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .as_slice()
    {
        [one] => Some(one),
        _ => None,
    }
}

impl Workflows {
    /// Whether the step run of `step` is still open; `Err` is the tool's answer. Called under
    /// the lock.
    async fn step_open(&self, run_id: &str, step: &StepTools) -> Result<(), String> {
        match self.store.get_step_run(&step.step_run_id).await {
            Ok(s) if matches!(s.status, StepRunStatus::Running | StepRunStatus::Waiting) => Ok(()),
            Ok(_) => Err(STEP_CLOSED.to_string()),
            Err(e) => {
                tracing::warn!(run = %run_id, "reading the step of a workflow tool failed: {e}");
                Err(format!("Not recorded: {e}."))
            }
        }
    }

    /// Announces the workflow run of `step` after a call changed its step run.
    async fn announce(&self, step: &StepTools) {
        let Ok(s) = self.store.get_step_run(&step.step_run_id).await else {
            return;
        };
        if let Ok(wr) = self.store.get_workflow_run(&s.workflow_run_id).await {
            self.updated(&wr);
        }
    }

    /// Performs an allowed `report_outcome` call of the run `run_id`, which works for the step
    /// of `step`: the label must be one of the step's outcomes. `Ok` is the text the model
    /// gets; `Err` a tool error, in one sentence, and the run goes on.
    pub async fn call_report_outcome(
        &self,
        run_id: &str,
        step: &StepTools,
        input: &Value,
    ) -> std::result::Result<String, String> {
        let _one = self.lock.lock().await;
        self.step_open(run_id, step).await?;
        let given = arg(input, "outcome");
        let Some(outcome) = outcome_of(&step.outcomes, &given) else {
            return Err(format!(
                "\"{given}\" is not an outcome of this step; use one of: {}.",
                quoted(step.outcomes.iter().map(String::as_str))
            ));
        };
        if let Err(e) = self
            .store
            .set_step_outcome(&step.step_run_id, Some(outcome))
            .await
        {
            tracing::warn!(run = %run_id, "recording an outcome failed: {e}");
            return Err(format!("Not recorded: {e}."));
        }
        self.announce(step).await;
        Ok("Outcome recorded.".to_string())
    }

    /// Performs an allowed `send_back` call of the run `run_id`, which works for the step of
    /// `step`: `to_step` names one of the steps it may send back to, without case, and the
    /// note is 1 to 8,000 characters. It is recorded on the step run, and performed when the
    /// step succeeds. `Ok` is the text the model gets; `Err` a tool error, in one sentence.
    pub async fn call_send_back(
        &self,
        run_id: &str,
        step: &StepTools,
        input: &Value,
    ) -> std::result::Result<String, String> {
        let _one = self.lock.lock().await;
        self.step_open(run_id, step).await?;
        let (to, note) = (arg(input, "to_step"), arg(input, "note"));
        let Some(target) = step
            .send_back_to
            .iter()
            .find(|t| t.name.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case(&to))
        else {
            return Err(format!(
                "You can send work back only to: {}.",
                quoted(step.send_back_to.iter().map(|t| t.name.as_str()))
            ));
        };
        if note.is_empty() || note.chars().count() > MAX_TASK {
            return Err("Give a note of 1 to 8,000 characters that says what to change.".into());
        }
        let send_back = json!({"to": target.step_id, "note": note});
        if let Err(e) = self
            .store
            .set_step_send_back(&step.step_run_id, Some(&send_back))
            .await
        {
            tracing::warn!(run = %run_id, "recording a send-back failed: {e}");
            return Err(format!("Not recorded: {e}."));
        }
        self.announce(step).await;
        Ok(format!(
            "Sent back to \"{}\". End your turn now: the work goes back when you finish.",
            target.name
        ))
    }
}
```

---

### Task 5: A delegate step starts other members and is resumed with their results

A delegate step's lead gets `start_member`. Each call starts a member's run (its own root, the step run id, a `## Workflow` section of its own, no workflow tools) from the step's start branch, and stores a `workflow_step_members` row with it. When the lead has ended and its members have ended, Dots continues the lead with their results, once; past a limit it waits on budget. A lead that fails cancels its running members. The hand-off gains `members`, and its branches follow spec 6.2.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/dots-core/src/store/step_members.rs`, `crates/dots-core/src/workflows/delegate.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dots-core/migrations/0027_teams_b.sql`, `crates/dots-core/src/{model.rs,team_tools.rs,workflows.rs,workflows/jobs.rs,workflows/tools.rs,store/mod.rs,server/mcp.rs}`, `crates/dots-core/src/engine/local/tools/{mod.rs,team.rs}`
- Test: `crates/dots-core/tests/{workflows_test,workflow_tools_test,store_workflow_runs_test}.rs`; brought up to date: `crates/dots-core/tests/local_tools_test.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4's `StepTools`, `RunTools::offers`, `for_run`, the `StartMember` tool; Task 3's `start_hand_off`; `checkins::continuation`.
- Produces: table `workflow_step_members (run_id, step_run_id, dot_id, task, reported, created_at)`; `model::{Handoff.members: Vec<MemberHandoff> (#[serde(default)]), MemberHandoff { dot_id, task, status: RunStatus, summary, branch: Option<String> }, StepMember { run_id, step_run_id, dot_id, task, reported: bool, created_at }}`; `Store::{add_step_member(&self, new: &NewRun, task: &str) -> Result<Run>, step_members(&self, step_run_id: &str) -> Result<Vec<StepMember>>, step_member(&self, root_run_id: &str) -> Result<Option<StepMember>>, report_step_members(&self, new: &NewRun, reported: &[String]) -> Result<Run>}`; `team_tools::{StartableMember { dot_id, name }, DelegateTools { may_start: Vec<StartableMember>, at_once: u32, total: u32 }, StepTools.delegate: Option<DelegateTools>, StepTools::read(store: &Store, step_run_id: &str, step: &Step, definition: &Definition) -> Result<Option<StepTools>>}`; `Workflows::call_start_member(&self, run_id: &str, step: &StepTools, input: &Value) -> std::result::Result<String, String>`; crate-private in `workflows/delegate.rs`: `own_runs(runs: &[Run], members: &[StepMember]) -> Vec<&Run>`, `member_running(runs, member) -> bool`, `member_section`, `members_done(wr, step, step_run, last: &Run, members: &[StepMember], runs: &[Run]) -> Result<bool>`, `member_end(wr, member, runs, commit: bool) -> (MemberHandoff, Option<BranchRef>)`, `cancel_members(runs, members)`; in `workflows.rs`: `branch_exists`, `has_commits`, `branch_ref`, `handoff_of(wr, members, runs)`.

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_member_is_added_with_its_run_and_reported_with_the_run_that_continues_the_step() {
    let f = fixture().await;
    let wr = f.store.create_workflow_run(&f.new).await.unwrap();
    let step = f
        .store
        .create_step_run(&wr.id, "a", 1, StepRunStatus::Running)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let mut lead = NewRun::new(&f.dot, TriggerKind::Workflow);
    lead.workflow_step_run_id = Some(step.id.clone());
    let lead = f.store.create_run(&lead).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(f.store.step_member(&lead.root_run_id).await.unwrap(), None);
    // A member's first run is its thread's root, and works for the step.
    let mut new = NewRun::new(&f.dot, TriggerKind::Workflow);
    new.workflow_step_run_id = Some(step.id.clone());
    let one = f.store.add_step_member(&new, "Part one.").await.unwrap();
    let two = f.store.add_step_member(&new, "Part two.").await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(one.root_run_id, one.id);
    assert_eq!(one.workflow_step_run_id.as_deref(), Some(step.id.as_str()));
    let members = f.store.step_members(&step.id).await.unwrap();
    let rows: Vec<_> = members
        .iter()
        .map(|m| {
            (
                m.run_id.as_str(),
                m.dot_id.as_str(),
                m.task.as_str(),
                m.reported,
            )
        })
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(
        rows,
        [
            (one.id.as_str(), f.dot.as_str(), "Part one.", false),
            (two.id.as_str(), f.dot.as_str(), "Part two.", false),
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        f.store.step_member(&one.id).await.unwrap().as_ref(),
        Some(&members[0])
    );
    // Both count against the budget, with the lead's run.
    assert_eq!(
        f.store.get_workflow_run(&wr.id).await.unwrap().spent_runs,
        3
    );
    // The run that tells the lead is stored with the members marked reported, once.
    let mut told = NewRun::new(&f.dot, TriggerKind::Chat);
    told.parent_run_id = Some(lead.id.clone());
    told.root_run_id = Some(lead.root_run_id.clone());
    told.workflow_step_run_id = Some(step.id.clone());
    let reported = vec![one.id.clone()];
    let run = f.store.report_step_members(&told, &reported).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(run.root_run_id, lead.id);
    let members = f.store.step_members(&step.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        members.iter().map(|m| m.reported).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        [true, false]
    );
    let both = vec![two.id.clone(), one.id.clone()];
    assert!(matches!(
        f.store.report_step_members(&told, &both).await,
        Err(Error::Conflict(_))
    ));
    // Nothing of the refused one was stored: the second member is still to report, and the
    // thread has no third run.
    assert!(!f.store.step_members(&step.id).await.unwrap()[1].reported);
    assert_eq!(f.store.list_thread(&lead.id).await.unwrap().len(), 2);
    // A step that has ended takes in no member.
    f.store
        .set_step_run(
            &step.id,
            &StepRunChange {
                status: Some(StepRunStatus::Cancelled),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        )
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(
        f.store.add_step_member(&new, "Part three.").await,
        Err(Error::Conflict(_))
    ));
    assert_eq!(f.store.step_members(&step.id).await.unwrap().len(), 2);
}
```
