```rust
/// A delegate step of `lead` that may start `member`, one at a time and once in all.
fn delegate_step(lead: &Dot, member: &Dot) -> Step {
    Step {
        id: "split".into(),
        name: "Split it".into(),
        kind: StepKind::Agent {
            member: lead.id.clone(),
            job: "Split it.".into(),
            can_send_back_to: vec![],
            outcomes: vec![],
            delegate: Some(Delegate {
                may_start: vec![member.id.clone()],
                at_once: 1,
                total: 1,
            }),
        },
    }
}

/// The text of the last message the person's side sent in a recorded request.
fn last_user(req: &Value) -> String {
    messages(req)
        .iter()
        .rev()
        .find(|m| m["role"] == "user")
        .and_then(|m| m["content"].as_str())
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .to_string()
}

/// The local engine: a delegate step's run is offered `start_member`, its call starts the
/// member, whose run has no workflow tool, and the lead's thread is continued with the
/// member's result.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_local_lead_starts_a_member_and_is_told_how_it_went() {
    let t = start(json!([])).await;
    let lead_llm = Llm::start(vec![
        call(
            "c1",
            "start_member",
            json!({"member": "DEV", "task": "Make the icon light."}),
        ),
        text("Started."),
        text("All in."),
    ])
    .await;
    let dev_llm = Llm::start(vec![text("Made it light.")]).await;
    let lead =
        t.rt.create_dot(local_spec(&t, &lead_llm.endpoint(), "lead"), None, None)
            .await
            .unwrap();
    let dev =
        t.rt.create_dot(local_spec(&t, &dev_llm.endpoint(), "dev"), None, None)
            .await
            .unwrap();
    let wr = run_steps(&t, &[&lead, &dev], vec![delegate_step(&lead, &dev)]).await;
    let wr = until(&t, &wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded).await;
    let reqs = lead_llm.requests();
    assert_eq!(reqs.len(), 3);
    assert_eq!(workflow_tools(&reqs[0]), ["start_member"]);
    let first = prompt(&reqs[0]);
    assert!(
        first.contains(
            "### Workflow tools\n\n- Start other members of the team on parts of the work with \
             `start_member`, one of: \"dev\". At most 1 may run at once, and 1 in all. End your \
             turn once you have started them: you are told when they finish.\n"
        ),
        "{first}"
    );
    assert_eq!(tool_result(&reqs[1], "c1"), "Started \"dev\".");
    // The member's run has no workflow tool, and its prompt names its part.
    let member = dev_llm.requests().remove(0);
    assert!(
        workflow_tools(&member).is_empty(),
        "{:?}",
        tool_names(&member)
    );
    let part = prompt(&member);
    assert!(
        part.contains("### Your part\n\nMake the icon light.")
            && !part.contains("### Workflow tools"),
        "{part}"
    );
    // The lead goes on in its thread, with its tool, told of the member.
    assert_eq!(workflow_tools(&reqs[2]), ["start_member"]);
    assert_eq!(
        last_user(&reqs[2]),
        "The members you started have finished:\n\n#### dev: Make the icon light.\n\n\
         Status: succeeded\nBranch: none\n\nMade it light.\n\n\
         You may start no more members: end with your summary of the step."
    );
    let handoff = wr.steps[0].handoff.clone().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(handoff.summary, "All in.");
    assert_eq!(handoff.members.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(handoff.members[0].summary, "Made it light.");
    t.rt.shutdown();
}

/// The Claude engine: Dots' own server lists `start_member` to a delegate step's run and
/// performs it, the CLI may call it without asking, and a call past `total` (the continued
/// run plays the same script) is refused.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_claude_lead_starts_a_member_through_dots_own_server() {
    let t = start(json!([
        {"emit": {"type": "system", "subtype": "init", "session_id": "$SESSION"}},
        {"mcp_list": "dots"},
        {"mcp_call": {"server": "dots", "tool": "start_member",
                      "arguments": {"member": "dev", "task": "Make it."}}},
        {"emit": {"type": "result", "subtype": "success", "is_error": false, "result": "done",
                  "usage": {"input_tokens": 1, "output_tokens": 1}}}
    ]))
    .await;
    let llm = Llm::start(vec![text("Made it.")]).await;
    let dev =
        t.rt.create_dot(local_spec(&t, &llm.endpoint(), "dev"), None, None)
            .await
            .unwrap();
    let mut s = spec("lead");
    s.workdir = t.dir.path().to_string_lossy().to_string();
    s.workspace_mode = WorkspaceMode::Folder;
    s.policy = Policy::preset(Preset::Trusted);
    let lead = t.rt.create_dot(s, None, None).await.unwrap();
    let wr = run_steps(&t, &[&lead, &dev], vec![delegate_step(&lead, &dev)]).await;
    let wr = until(&t, &wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded).await;
    let recorded: Value =
        serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(&t.args_out).unwrap()).unwrap();
    let args: Vec<String> = serde_json::from_value(recorded["args"].clone()).unwrap();
    let allowed: Vec<&str> = args
        .iter()
        .skip_while(|a| *a != "--allowedTools")
        .skip(1)
        .take_while(|a| !a.starts_with("--"))
        .map(String::as_str)
        .filter(|a| {
            WORKFLOW_TOOLS
                .iter()
                .any(|t| *a == format!("mcp__dots__{t}"))
        })
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(allowed, ["mcp__dots__start_member"], "{args:?}");
    // The args are the continued run's: its prompt is the member's result.
    let told = recorded["prompt"].as_str().unwrap();
    assert!(
        told.contains("#### dev: Make it.\n\nStatus: succeeded\nBranch: none\n\nMade it.\n\n"),
        "{told}"
    );
    let runs =
        t.rt.store()
            .runs_of_step_run(&wr.steps[0].id)
            .await
            .unwrap();
    let members = t.rt.store().step_members(&wr.steps[0].id).await.unwrap();
    let own: Vec<_> = runs
        .iter()
        .filter(|r| !members.iter().any(|m| m.run_id == r.root_run_id))
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(own.len(), 2);
    let mut answers = Vec::new();
    for run in &own {
        let events = t.rt.store().list_events(&run.id, 0).await.unwrap();
        let listed = events.iter().find(|e| e.kind == "raw").unwrap();
        let listed: Value = serde_json::from_str(listed.data["line"].as_str().unwrap()).unwrap();
        let listed: Vec<&str> = listed["tools"]
            .as_array()
            .unwrap()
            .iter()
            .filter_map(Value::as_str)
            .filter(|n| WORKFLOW_TOOLS.contains(n))
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(listed, ["start_member"]);
        answers.extend(events.iter().filter(|e| e.kind == "tool_result").map(|e| {
            (
                e.data["output"].as_str().unwrap().to_string(),
                e.data["is_error"].as_bool().unwrap(),
            )
        }));
    }
    assert_eq!(
        answers,
        [
            ("Started \"dev\".".to_string(), false),
            (
                "Not started: you have started 1 members, the most this step allows.".to_string(),
                true
            ),
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(members.len(), 1);
    t.rt.shutdown();
}
```

```rust
/// A delegate step of `lead` that may start `may_start`, `at_once` at a time and `total` in
/// all.
fn delegating(
    id: &str,
    name: &str,
    lead: &Dot,
    may_start: &[&Dot],
    at_once: u32,
    total: u32,
) -> Step {
    Step {
        id: id.into(),
        name: name.into(),
        kind: StepKind::Agent {
            member: lead.id.clone(),
            job: "Split the work.".into(),
            can_send_back_to: vec![],
            outcomes: vec![],
            delegate: Some(Delegate {
                may_start: may_start.iter().map(|d| d.id.clone()).collect(),
                at_once,
                total,
            }),
        },
    }
}

impl World {
    /// The id of the step run of `step` once the workflow run `id` has one.
    async fn step_run_of(&self, id: &str, step: &str) -> String {
        self.until(id, |wr| wr.steps.iter().any(|s| s.step_id == step))
            .await
            .steps
            .iter()
            .find(|s| s.step_id == step)
            .unwrap()
            .id
            .clone()
    }

    /// The first runs of the members the step run `step` started, in the order started, once
    /// there are `n` of them and each is `status` (a running one, once its workspace is made).
    async fn members_are(&self, step: &str, n: usize, status: RunStatus) -> Vec<Run> {
        let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(60);
        loop {
            let mut runs = Vec::new();
            for m in self.store.step_members(step).await.unwrap() {
                runs.push(self.store.get_run(&m.run_id).await.unwrap());
            }
            let ready = |r: &Run| {
                r.status == status && (status != RunStatus::Running || r.workspace_path.is_some())
            };
            if runs.len() == n && runs.iter().all(ready) {
                return runs;
            }
            assert!(
                std::time::Instant::now() < deadline,
                "the members are not {status:?}: {runs:#?}"
            );
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(30)).await;
        }
    }

    /// The first run of the step run `step`'s own thread, once it is `status`.
    async fn lead_is(&self, step: &str, status: RunStatus) -> Run {
        let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(60);
        loop {
            let runs = self.store.runs_of_step_run(step).await.unwrap();
            if let Some(run) = runs.first().filter(|r| r.status == status) {
                return run.clone();
            }
            assert!(
                std::time::Instant::now() < deadline,
                "the lead is not {status:?}: {runs:#?}"
            );
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(30)).await;
        }
    }
}

/// What a member's thread ended with, as the hand-off has it.
fn handed(
    dot: &Dot,
    task: &str,
    status: RunStatus,
    summary: &str,
    branch: Option<&str>,
) -> MemberHandoff {
    MemberHandoff {
        dot_id: dot.id.clone(),
        task: task.into(),
        status,
        summary: summary.into(),
        branch: branch.map(str::to_string),
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_delegate_step_runs_its_members_at_once_and_is_continued_with_their_results() {
    let w = world().await;
    let light = "Make the icon light.\nUse the light set.";
    w.engine.set(
        &w.helper.spec.name,
        vec![
            Act::Write("plan.txt", "The plan."),
            Act::Start(
                vec![("Dev", light), ("dev", "Make the menu dark.")],
                Box::new(Act::Finish("Started two.")),
            ),
            Act::Finish("Both are in."),
        ],
    );
    w.engine.set(
        &w.dev.spec.name,
        vec![Act::Await("part.txt", "Did my part.")],
    );
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Fix",
            vec![
                agent("plan", "Plan it", &w.helper, "Plan."),
                delegating("split", "Split it", &w.helper, &[&w.dev], 3, 6),
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let split = w.step_run_of(&wr.id, "split").await;
    // Both members run at once, each in a worktree of its own, while the lead's run has
    // ended and the step waits for them.
    let members = w.members_are(&split, 2, RunStatus::Running).await;
    assert_ne!(members[0].workspace_path, members[1].workspace_path);
    assert_ne!(members[0].branch, members[1].branch);
    w.lead_is(&split, RunStatus::Succeeded).await;
    w.workflows.pass().await.unwrap();
    let held = w.store.get_workflow_run(&wr.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(held.steps[1].status, StepRunStatus::Running);
    assert_eq!(w.engine.prompts_of("helper").len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(
        w.engine.answers(),
        [
            Ok("Started \"dev\".".to_string()),
            Ok("Started \"dev\".".to_string())
        ]
    );
    w.engine.held.add_permits(2);
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    let b: Vec<String> = members.iter().map(|m| m.branch.clone().unwrap()).collect();
    // The lead is continued once, with both results.
    let helper = w.engine.prompts_of("helper");
    assert_eq!(helper.len(), 3);
    assert_eq!(
        helper[2],
        format!(
            "The members you started have finished:\n\n\
             #### dev: Make the icon light.\n\nStatus: succeeded\nBranch: {}\n\nDid my part.\n\n\
             #### dev: Make the menu dark.\n\nStatus: succeeded\nBranch: {}\n\nDid my part.\n\n\
             You may start 4 more with `mcp__dots__start_member`, or end with your summary of \
             the step.",
            b[0], b[1]
        )
    );
    // The lead's prompt names its tool; the members' name their part, and they have no tools.
    assert!(
        helper[1].contains(
            "### Workflow tools\n\n- Start other members of the team on parts of the work with \
             `mcp__dots__start_member`, one of: \"dev\". At most 3 may run at once, and 6 in all. \
             End your turn once you have started them: you are told when they finish.\n\n\
             When you are done"
        ),
        "{}",
        helper[1]
    );
    let lead_tools = w.engine.step_tools_of("helper")[1].clone().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        lead_tools.delegate,
        Some(DelegateTools {
            may_start: vec![StartableMember {
                dot_id: w.dev.id.clone(),
                name: "dev".into(),
            }],
            at_once: 3,
            total: 6,
        })
    );
    assert_eq!(w.engine.step_tools_of("dev"), [None, None]);
    for prompt in w.engine.prompts_of("dev") {
        for part in [
            "## Workflow\n\nYou are the dev agent of the team \"Dots management\". helper started \
             you on part of one step of the workflow \"Fix\".",
            "- Step: Split it (2 of 2)",
            "### Task\n\nThe tray icon is invisible in dark mode.",
            "When you are done, end with a summary of what you did: helper gets it.",
        ] {
            assert!(prompt.contains(part), "missing {part:?} in:\n{prompt}");
        }
        assert!(!prompt.contains("### Workflow tools"), "{prompt}");
    }
    assert!(w
        .engine
        .prompts_of("dev")
        .iter()
        .any(|p| p.contains(&format!("### Your part\n\n{light}\n\n"))));
    // Both started from the step's start, the plan's branch, and kept their work.
    for branch in &b {
        assert_eq!(
            git_out(&w.repo, &["show", &format!("{branch}:plan.txt")]),
            "The plan."
        );
        assert_eq!(
            git_out(&w.repo, &["show", &format!("{branch}:part.txt")]),
            "Did my part."
        );
    }
    // The lead made no change: the hand-off carries the members' branches.
    let handoff = wr.steps[1].handoff.clone().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(handoff.summary, "Both are in.");
    assert_eq!(
        handoff.members,
        [
            handed(
                &w.dev,
                light,
                RunStatus::Succeeded,
                "Did my part.",
                Some(&b[0])
            ),
            handed(
                &w.dev,
                "Make the menu dark.",
                RunStatus::Succeeded,
                "Did my part.",
                Some(&b[1])
            ),
        ]
    );
    let branches: Vec<&str> = handoff.branches.iter().map(|r| r.branch.as_str()).collect();
    assert_eq!(branches, [b[0].as_str(), b[1].as_str()]);
    assert_eq!(handoff.branches[0].run_id, members[0].id);
    // The plan's run, the lead's two and the members' two.
    assert_eq!(wr.spent_runs, 5);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_lead_that_merged_its_members_work_hands_on_its_own_branch_alone() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.helper.spec.name,
        vec![
            Act::Start(
                vec![("dev", "Part one.")],
                Box::new(Act::Finish("Started.")),
            ),
            Act::Write("merged.txt", "Merged."),
        ],
    );
    w.engine
        .set(&w.dev.spec.name, vec![Act::Write("part.txt", "Did it.")]);
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Fix",
            vec![delegating("split", "Split it", &w.helper, &[&w.dev], 3, 6)],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    let split = &wr.steps[0];
    let members = w.members_are(&split.id, 1, RunStatus::Succeeded).await;
    let lead = w.lead_is(&split.id, RunStatus::Succeeded).await;
    let handoff = split.handoff.clone().unwrap();
    let own = lead.branch.clone().unwrap();
    let branches: Vec<&str> = handoff.branches.iter().map(|r| r.branch.as_str()).collect();
    assert_eq!(branches, [own.as_str()]);
    assert_eq!(
        git_out(&w.repo, &["show", &format!("{own}:merged.txt")]),
        "Merged."
    );
    // The member's branch is still named in its part of the hand-off.
    let member_branch = members[0].branch.clone().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        handoff.members,
        [handed(
            &w.dev,
            "Part one.",
            RunStatus::Succeeded,
            "Did it.",
            Some(&member_branch)
        )]
    );
    assert_eq!(handoff.summary, "Merged.");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn start_member_refuses_other_dots_bad_tasks_and_more_than_its_limits_allow() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.helper.spec.name,
        vec![
            Act::Start(
                vec![
                    ("Helper", "Do it."),
                    ("dev", "  "),
                    ("DEV", "Part one."),
                    ("dev", "Part two."),
                ],
                Box::new(Act::Finish("Started one.")),
            ),
            Act::Start(
                vec![("dev", "Part two.")],
                Box::new(Act::Finish("Started two.")),
            ),
            Act::Start(
                vec![("dev", "Part three.")],
                Box::new(Act::Finish("All in.")),
            ),
        ],
    );
    w.engine
        .set(&w.dev.spec.name, vec![Act::Await("part.txt", "Did it.")]);
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Fix",
            vec![delegating("split", "Split it", &w.helper, &[&w.dev], 1, 2)],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let split = w.step_run_of(&wr.id, "split").await;
    // The first member holds until the lead's run has made its calls.
    w.lead_is(&split, RunStatus::Succeeded).await;
    w.engine.held.add_permits(2);
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    let started = Ok("Started \"dev\".".to_string());
    assert_eq!(
        w.engine.answers(),
        [
            Err("\"Helper\" is not a member you may start; you may start: \"dev\".".to_string()),
            Err(
                "Give a task of 1 to 8,000 characters that says what the member should do."
                    .to_string()
            ),
            started.clone(),
            Err(
                "Not started: 1 members you started are still running, and at most 1 may run \
                 at once. End your turn; you will be told when they finish."
                    .to_string()
            ),
            started,
            Err("Not started: you have started 2 members, the most this step allows.".to_string()),
        ]
    );
    let helper = w.engine.prompts_of("helper");
    assert_eq!(helper.len(), 3);
    assert!(
        helper[1].contains("#### dev: Part one.\n\nStatus: succeeded\n")
            && helper[1].ends_with(
                "You may start 1 more with `mcp__dots__start_member`, or end with your summary \
                 of the step."
            ),
        "{}",
        helper[1]
    );
    assert!(
        helper[2].contains("#### dev: Part two.\n\nStatus: succeeded\n")
            && !helper[2].contains("Part one.")
            && helper[2]
                .ends_with("You may start no more members: end with your summary of the step."),
        "{}",
        helper[2]
    );
    let handoff = wr.steps[0].handoff.clone().unwrap();
    let tasks: Vec<&str> = handoff.members.iter().map(|m| m.task.as_str()).collect();
    assert_eq!(tasks, ["Part one.", "Part two."]);
    assert_eq!(handoff.summary, "All in.");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_member_past_the_budget_is_refused_and_the_lead_waits_on_budget_to_go_on() {
    let budget = Budget {
        max_runs: 2,
        ..Budget::default()
    };
    let w = world_with(Arc::new(SystemClock), budget).await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.helper.spec.name,
        vec![
            Act::Start(
                vec![("dev", "Part one."), ("dev", "Part two.")],
                Box::new(Act::Finish("Started.")),
            ),
            Act::Finish("Done."),
        ],
    );
    w.engine
        .set(&w.dev.spec.name, vec![Act::Fail("no disk left")]);
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Fix",
            vec![delegating("split", "Split it", &w.helper, &[&w.dev], 3, 6)],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    // The lead's run and the first member's spent the two runs.
    let held = w.until(&wr.id, waits_on(WaitingOn::Budget)).await;
    assert_eq!(held.error.as_deref(), Some("Budget reached: runs, 2 of 2"));
    assert_eq!(held.steps[0].status, StepRunStatus::Running);
    assert_eq!(
        w.engine.answers(),
        [
            Ok("Started \"dev\".".to_string()),
            Err("Not started: Budget reached: runs, 2 of 2.".to_string()),
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(w.engine.prompts_of("helper").len(), 1);
    // More budget lets the lead go on, told of the member that failed: not fatal to the step.
    w.workflows.add_budget(&wr.id).await.unwrap();
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    let told = &w.engine.prompts_of("helper")[1];
    assert!(
        told.contains("#### dev: Part one.\n\nStatus: failed\nBranch: none\n\nno disk left\n\n"),
        "{told}"
    );
    let handoff = wr.steps[0].handoff.clone().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        handoff.members,
        [handed(
            &w.dev,
            "Part one.",
            RunStatus::Failed,
            "no disk left",
            None
        )]
    );
    assert!(handoff.branches.is_empty());
    assert_eq!(wr.spent_runs, 3);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_lead_that_fails_cancels_its_running_members_and_fails_the_step() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.helper.spec.name,
        vec![Act::Start(
            vec![("dev", "Part one.")],
            Box::new(Act::Fail("lost the plan")),
        )],
    );
    w.engine.set(&w.dev.spec.name, vec![Act::Hold]);
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Fix",
            vec![delegating("split", "Split it", &w.helper, &[&w.dev], 3, 6)],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, waits_on(WaitingOn::StepFailed)).await;
    assert_eq!(wr.error.as_deref(), Some("Split it: lost the plan"));
    assert_eq!(wr.steps[0].status, StepRunStatus::Failed);
    let member = w.store.step_members(&wr.steps[0].id).await.unwrap()[0]
        .run_id
        .clone();
    common::wait_status(&w.store, &member, RunStatus::Cancelled, 60).await;
    assert_eq!(w.engine.prompts_of("helper").len(), 1);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn stopping_a_delegate_step_cancels_its_members() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.helper.spec.name,
        vec![Act::Start(
            vec![("dev", "Part one.")],
            Box::new(Act::Finish("Started.")),
        )],
    );
    w.engine.set(&w.dev.spec.name, vec![Act::Hold]);
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Fix",
            vec![delegating("split", "Split it", &w.helper, &[&w.dev], 3, 6)],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let split = w.step_run_of(&wr.id, "split").await;
    let member = w.members_are(&split, 1, RunStatus::Running).await.remove(0);
    let stopped = w.workflows.stop(&wr.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(stopped.status, WorkflowRunStatus::Cancelled);
    assert_eq!(stopped.steps[0].status, StepRunStatus::Cancelled);
    common::wait_status(&w.store, &member.id, RunStatus::Cancelled, 60).await;
    assert_eq!(w.engine.prompts_of("helper").len(), 1);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_member_interrupted_by_a_restart_is_reported_as_failed_and_the_lead_goes_on() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.helper.spec.name,
        vec![
            Act::Start(
                vec![("dev", "Part one.")],
                Box::new(Act::Finish("Started.")),
            ),
            Act::Finish("Went on without it."),
        ],
    );
    w.engine.set(&w.dev.spec.name, vec![Act::Hold]);
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Fix",
            vec![delegating("split", "Split it", &w.helper, &[&w.dev], 3, 6)],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let split = w.step_run_of(&wr.id, "split").await;
    let member = w.members_are(&split, 1, RunStatus::Running).await.remove(0);
    w.lead_is(&split, RunStatus::Succeeded).await;
    // As Dots does at start after a crash: the running member is failed as interrupted, and
    // a new loop's first pass continues the lead, which waited for it.
    let interrupted = w.store.recover_interrupted().await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(interrupted, std::slice::from_ref(&member.id));
    let fresh = Workflows::new(
        w.store.clone(),
        new_bus(),
        w.runner.clone(),
        Arc::new(SystemClock),
    );
    fresh.pass().await.unwrap();
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    let helper = w.engine.prompts_of("helper");
    assert_eq!(helper.len(), 2, "the lead was continued once");
    assert!(
        helper[1].contains("#### dev: Part one.\n\nStatus: failed\nBranch: ")
            && helper[1].contains("\n\ninterrupted\n\n"),
        "{}",
        helper[1]
    );
    let handoff = wr.steps[0].handoff.clone().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(handoff.summary, "Went on without it.");
    assert_eq!(handoff.members.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(handoff.members[0].status, RunStatus::Failed);
    assert_eq!(handoff.members[0].summary, "interrupted");
}
```

**Create `crates/dots-core/src/store/step_members.rs`:**

```rust
//! The members a delegate step's run started (teams spec section 5.3): a row per member
//! thread, added with the thread's first run and marked reported with the run that tells the
//! delegating run how it ended.

use sqlx::sqlite::SqliteRow;
use sqlx::Row;

use super::runs::insert_run;
use super::Store;
use crate::model::{NewRun, Run, StepMember};
use crate::util::now;
use crate::{Error, Result};

const COLUMNS: &str = "run_id, step_run_id, dot_id, task, reported, created_at";

fn member_from_row(row: &SqliteRow) -> Result<StepMember> {
    Ok(StepMember {
        run_id: row.try_get("run_id")?,
        step_run_id: row.try_get("step_run_id")?,
        dot_id: row.try_get("dot_id")?,
        task: row.try_get("task")?,
        reported: row.try_get::<i64, _>("reported")? != 0,
        created_at: row.try_get("created_at")?,
    })
}

impl Store {
    /// Stores the first run of a member's thread, `new` (its own root, with the delegate step's
    /// run as its `workflow_step_run_id`), and its member row with `task`, in one transaction.
    /// `Conflict` when the step run has ended meanwhile: nothing is stored.
    pub async fn add_step_member(&self, new: &NewRun, task: &str) -> Result<Run> {
        let Some(step_run_id) = &new.workflow_step_run_id else {
            return Err(Error::Invalid(
                "a member's run must name its step run".into(),
            ));
        };
        let mut tx = self.pool.begin().await?;
        // The write first: a transaction that reads first cannot take the write lock while
        // another connection writes ("database is locked").
        let run_id = insert_run(&mut tx, new).await?;
        let open: bool = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM workflow_step_runs WHERE id = ? \
             AND status IN ('running', 'waiting'))",
        )
        .bind(step_run_id)
        .fetch_one(&mut *tx)
        .await?;
        if !open {
            tx.rollback().await?;
            return Err(Error::Conflict(format!("step run {step_run_id} has ended")));
        }
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO workflow_step_members (run_id, step_run_id, dot_id, task, created_at) \
             VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        )
        .bind(&run_id)
        .bind(step_run_id)
        .bind(&new.dot_id)
        .bind(task)
        .bind(now())
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await?;
        tx.commit().await?;
        self.get_run(&run_id).await
    }

    /// The members the step run's delegating run started, in the order started.
    pub async fn step_members(&self, step_run_id: &str) -> Result<Vec<StepMember>> {
        sqlx::query(&format!(
            "SELECT {COLUMNS} FROM workflow_step_members WHERE step_run_id = ? \
             ORDER BY created_at, rowid"
        ))
        .bind(step_run_id)
        .fetch_all(&self.pool)
        .await?
        .iter()
        .map(member_from_row)
        .collect()
    }

    /// The member whose thread has the root run `root_run_id`, if it is a member's thread.
    pub async fn step_member(&self, root_run_id: &str) -> Result<Option<StepMember>> {
        sqlx::query(&format!(
            "SELECT {COLUMNS} FROM workflow_step_members WHERE run_id = ?"
        ))
        .bind(root_run_id)
        .fetch_optional(&self.pool)
        .await?
        .as_ref()
        .map(member_from_row)
        .transpose()
    }

    /// Stores `new`, the run that continues the delegating thread with the results of the
    /// members whose threads have the roots `reported`, and marks those members reported, in
    /// one transaction. `Conflict` when one of them was reported meanwhile (a second pass, or
    /// a second loop after a restart): nothing is stored.
    pub async fn report_step_members(&self, new: &NewRun, reported: &[String]) -> Result<Run> {
        let mut tx = self.pool.begin().await?;
        for run_id in reported {
            let done = sqlx::query(
                "UPDATE workflow_step_members SET reported = 1 WHERE run_id = ? AND reported = 0",
            )
            .bind(run_id)
            .execute(&mut *tx)
            .await?;
            if done.rows_affected() == 0 {
                tx.rollback().await?;
                return Err(Error::Conflict(format!(
                    "the member {run_id} was reported already"
                )));
            }
        }
        let run_id = insert_run(&mut tx, new).await?;
        tx.commit().await?;
        self.get_run(&run_id).await
    }
}
```

```rust

    /// `of`, with the members a delegate step may start named as their dots are now. A member
    /// that is no longer a dot is left out: it could not be started.
    pub async fn read(
        store: &Store,
        step_run_id: &str,
        step: &Step,
        definition: &Definition,
    ) -> Result<Option<StepTools>> {
        let Some(mut tools) = StepTools::of(step_run_id, step, definition) else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        if let Some(delegate) = &mut tools.delegate {
            let mut named = Vec::new();
            for member in &delegate.may_start {
                match store.get_dot(&member.dot_id).await {
                    Ok(dot) => named.push(StartableMember {
                        dot_id: dot.id,
                        name: dot.spec.name,
                    }),
                    Err(crate::Error::NotFound(_)) => {}
                    Err(e) => return Err(e),
                }
            }
            delegate.may_start = named;
        }
        Ok(Some(tools))
    }

    /// Whether the run has `tool`: `report_outcome` with outcomes to report, `send_back` with
    /// steps to send back to, `start_member` with members to start. Never `start_workflow`,
    /// which is a chat's tool.
```

```rust
/// Where a step's prompt places it: the project, and the step with its number among all the
/// steps of the tree, branches and the paths not taken included.
fn place(wr: &WorkflowRun, step: &Step) -> String {
    format!(
        "- Project: {} ({})\n- Step: {} ({} of {})\n\n",
        wr.project.name,
        wr.project.repo_path,
        step.name,
        wr.definition.position(&step.id).unwrap_or_default() + 1,
        wr.definition.all().len()
    )
}

/// Whether the branch `branch` exists in `repo`, as a branch (a tag of that name is not it).
async fn branch_exists(repo: &Path, branch: &str) -> bool {
    git(
        repo,
        &[
            "rev-parse",
            "--verify",
            "--quiet",
            "--end-of-options",
            &format!("refs/heads/{branch}"),
        ],
    )
    .await
    .is_ok()
}

/// Whether the branch `branch` has commits since `base`, the commit its worktree started on;
/// without a base, whether it exists.
async fn has_commits(repo: &Path, branch: &str, base: Option<&str>) -> bool {
    let Some(base) = base else {
        return branch_exists(repo, branch).await;
    };
    let range = format!("{base}..refs/heads/{branch}");
    git(repo, &["rev-list", "--count", "--end-of-options", &range])
        .await
        .is_ok_and(|n| n.trim().parse::<u64>().is_ok_and(|n| n > 0))
}

/// The hand-off's reference to `branch`, the branch of `run`.
fn branch_ref(run: &Run, branch: String) -> BranchRef {
    BranchRef {
        run_id: run.id.clone(),
        branch,
        worktree_path: run.workspace_path.clone().unwrap_or_default(),
        base_commit: run.base_commit.clone(),
    }
}
```

**Create `crates/dots-core/src/workflows/delegate.rs`:**

```rust
//! Delegate steps (teams spec section 5.3): the step's own run starts other members of the team
//! with `start_member` (`tools`), each in a thread of its own that works for the same step run.
//! A member's thread is the one whose root run has a `workflow_step_members` row; the step's
//! own thread is the rest of its runs. The step ends when its own thread and every member's
//! have ended and every member has been reported: once the own thread has ended well and the
//! members it is waiting for have all ended, Dots continues the own thread with their results,
//! as an answer to `ask` continues a thread.

use std::path::Path;

use super::{
    branch_exists, branch_ref, budget_reached, cut, place, Workflows, MAX_HANDOFF_SUMMARY,
};
use crate::model::{
    BranchRef, EngineKind, MemberHandoff, Run, RunStatus, StepMember, StepRun, WorkflowRun,
};
use crate::team_tools::Tool;
use crate::workflow::{Step, StepKind};
use crate::{Error, Result};

/// The most characters of a member's task the delegating run is reminded of, per member.
const MAX_TASK_LINE: usize = 80;

/// The runs of the step's own thread among `runs`, oldest first: those in no member's thread.
pub(super) fn own_runs<'a>(runs: &'a [Run], members: &[StepMember]) -> Vec<&'a Run> {
    runs.iter()
        .filter(|r| !members.iter().any(|m| m.run_id == r.root_run_id))
        .collect()
}

/// The runs of `member`'s thread among `runs`, oldest first.
fn thread_of<'a>(runs: &'a [Run], member: &StepMember) -> Vec<&'a Run> {
    runs.iter()
        .filter(|r| r.root_run_id == member.run_id)
        .collect()
}

/// Whether the thread of `member` has a run that has not ended (queued ones included).
pub(super) fn member_running(runs: &[Run], member: &StepMember) -> bool {
    thread_of(runs, member)
        .iter()
        .any(|r| !r.status.is_terminal())
}

impl Workflows {
    /// The `## Workflow` section of the prompt of a member that the delegating run of `step`
    /// started on `task` (section 5.3): the team and its role, the workflow's task, its part
    /// of it, the project, and who started it. It names no workflow tools: a member has none.
    pub(super) async fn member_section(
        &self,
        wr: &WorkflowRun,
        step: &Step,
        member: &str,
        task: &str,
    ) -> Result<String> {
        let (team, you, workflow) = self.names(wr, member).await?;
        let starter = match &step.kind {
            StepKind::Agent { member, .. } => self.store.get_dot(member).await.ok(),
            _ => None,
        }
        .map_or_else(|| "a member".to_string(), |d| d.spec.name);
        let mut out = String::from("## Workflow\n\n");
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "You are {you} of the team \"{team}\". {starter} started you on part of one step of \
             the workflow \"{workflow}\".\n\n"
        ));
        out.push_str(&place(wr, step));
        out.push_str("### Task\n\n");
        out.push_str(wr.task.trim());
        out.push_str("\n\n### Your part\n\n");
        out.push_str(task.trim());
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "\n\nWhen you are done, end with a summary of what you did: {starter} gets it.\n"
        ));
        Ok(out)
    }

    /// Cancels the runs of the members' threads that have not ended: the delegating thread
    /// they work for failed. A cancel that fails is logged.
    pub(super) async fn cancel_members(&self, runs: &[Run], members: &[StepMember]) {
        for member in members {
            for run in thread_of(runs, member) {
                if run.status.is_terminal() {
                    continue;
                }
                if let Err(e) = self.runner.cancel(&run.id).await {
                    tracing::warn!(run = %run.id, "cancelling a member's run failed: {e}");
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// For a delegate step whose own thread has ended with `last` succeeded: whether its
    /// members are done, every one ended and reported, so the step may end. Until then it
    /// waits while a member runs; once none runs, it continues the own thread with the results
    /// of those not reported yet, or past a limit waits on budget (section 9) and continues it
    /// when the person adds some.
    pub(super) async fn members_done(
        &self,
        wr: &WorkflowRun,
        step: &Step,
        step_run: &StepRun,
        last: &Run,
        members: &[StepMember],
        runs: &[Run],
    ) -> Result<bool> {
        if members.iter().any(|m| member_running(runs, m)) {
            return Ok(false);
        }
        let unreported: Vec<&StepMember> = members.iter().filter(|m| !m.reported).collect();
        if unreported.is_empty() {
            return Ok(true);
        }
        if let Some(limit) = budget_reached(wr) {
            self.wait_on_budget(wr, &limit).await?;
            return Ok(false);
        }
        let mut message = String::from("The members you started have finished:\n\n");
        for member in &unreported {
            let (end, _) = self.member_end(wr, member, runs, false).await;
            let name = match self.store.get_dot(&member.dot_id).await {
                Ok(dot) => dot.spec.name,
                Err(_) => member.dot_id.clone(),
            };
            let first = member.task.lines().next().unwrap_or_default().trim();
            message.push_str(&format!(
                "#### {name}: {}\n\nStatus: {}\nBranch: {}\n\n{}\n\n",
                cut(first, MAX_TASK_LINE),
                end.status.as_str(),
                end.branch.as_deref().unwrap_or("none"),
                end.summary
            ));
        }
        let total = match &step.kind {
            StepKind::Agent {
                delegate: Some(d), ..
            } => d.total,
            _ => 0,
        };
        let left = total.saturating_sub(members.len() as u32);
        // A lead that is no longer a dot fails its continued run, and with it the step.
        let engine = match self.store.get_dot(&last.dot_id).await {
            Ok(dot) => dot.spec.engine,
            Err(Error::NotFound(_)) => EngineKind::Local,
            Err(e) => return Err(e),
        };
        let tool = Tool::StartMember.model_name(engine);
        message.push_str(&if left > 0 {
            format!(
                "You may start {left} more with `{tool}`, or end with your summary of the step."
            )
        } else {
            "You may start no more members: end with your summary of the step.".to_string()
        });
        // The own thread goes on where it ended: the same session, worktree and branch. It
        // names the step run, as a run whose step has not ended does.
        let mut new = crate::checkins::continuation(last, last, message, None);
        new.workflow_step_run_id = Some(step_run.id.clone());
        let reported: Vec<String> = unreported.iter().map(|m| m.run_id.clone()).collect();
        let run = self.store.report_step_members(&new, &reported).await?;
        self.runner.enqueued(&run);
        self.updated(&self.store.get_workflow_run(&wr.id).await?);
        Ok(false)
    }

    /// What `member`'s thread, which has ended, hands back (section 6): how its newest run
    /// ended, its summary (else its error) cut to the hand-off limit, and its branch while that
    /// exists, with the branch's reference for the step's hand-off. With `commit`, what its run
    /// left uncommitted is committed first, as for the step's own thread (section 6.1); the
    /// run's end has done it already unless Dots stopped in between.
    pub(super) async fn member_end(
        &self,
        wr: &WorkflowRun,
        member: &StepMember,
        runs: &[Run],
        commit: bool,
    ) -> (MemberHandoff, Option<BranchRef>) {
        let thread = thread_of(runs, member);
        let last = thread.last();
        let status = match last.map(|r| r.status) {
            Some(RunStatus::Succeeded) => RunStatus::Succeeded,
            Some(RunStatus::Cancelled) => RunStatus::Cancelled,
            _ => RunStatus::Failed,
        };
        let text = last
            .and_then(|r| r.summary.clone())
            .map(|s| s.trim().to_string())
            .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
            .or_else(|| last.and_then(|r| r.error.clone()))
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "(no summary)".to_string());
        let mut branch = None;
        if let Some(run) = thread.iter().rev().find(|r| r.branch.is_some()) {
            if commit {
                self.runner.commit_step_work(run).await;
            }
            let name = run.branch.clone().unwrap_or_default();
            if branch_exists(Path::new(&wr.project.repo_path), &name).await {
                branch = Some(branch_ref(run, name));
            }
        }
        let end = MemberHandoff {
            dot_id: member.dot_id.clone(),
            task: member.task.clone(),
            status,
            summary: cut(&text, MAX_HANDOFF_SUMMARY),
            branch: branch.as_ref().map(|b| b.branch.clone()),
        };
        (end, branch)
    }
}
```

```rust
    /// Performs an allowed `start_member` call of the run `run_id`, which works for the
    /// delegate step of `step` (section 5.3): `member` names one of the members it may start,
    /// without case, and `task` is 1 to 8,000 characters. Refused past the step's `total`, while
    /// `at_once` of its members run, and past a limit of the budget (section 9). The member's
    /// run is queued in a thread of its own, from the step's own start. `Ok` is the text the
    /// model gets; `Err` a tool error, in one sentence, and the run goes on.
    pub async fn call_start_member(
        &self,
        run_id: &str,
        step: &StepTools,
        input: &Value,
    ) -> std::result::Result<String, String> {
        let _one = self.lock.lock().await;
        self.step_open(run_id, step).await?;
        let Some(delegate) = &step.delegate else {
            return Err(format!(
                "{} is not available in this run",
                Tool::StartMember.name()
            ));
        };
        let (name, task) = (arg(input, "member"), arg(input, "task"));
        let Some(member) = delegate
            .may_start
            .iter()
            .find(|m| m.name.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case(&name))
        else {
            return Err(format!(
                "\"{name}\" is not a member you may start; you may start: {}.",
                quoted(delegate.may_start.iter().map(|m| m.name.as_str()))
            ));
        };
        if task.is_empty() || task.chars().count() > MAX_TASK {
            return Err(
                "Give a task of 1 to 8,000 characters that says what the member should do.".into(),
            );
        }
        match self.start_member(step, delegate, member, &task).await {
            Ok(answer) => answer,
            Err(e) => {
                tracing::warn!(run = %run_id, "starting a member failed: {e}");
                Err(format!("Not started: {e}."))
            }
        }
    }

    /// Starts `member` on `task` for the delegate step of `step`, unless a limit refuses it
    /// (the inner `Err`, the tool's answer). Called under the lock, by `call_start_member`.
    async fn start_member(
        &self,
        step: &StepTools,
        delegate: &DelegateTools,
        member: &StartableMember,
        task: &str,
    ) -> Result<std::result::Result<String, String>> {
        let members = self.store.step_members(&step.step_run_id).await?;
        if members.len() as u32 >= delegate.total {
            return Ok(Err(format!(
                "Not started: you have started {} members, the most this step allows.",
                delegate.total
            )));
        }
        let runs = self.store.runs_of_step_run(&step.step_run_id).await?;
        let running = members.iter().filter(|m| member_running(&runs, m)).count();
        if running as u32 >= delegate.at_once {
            return Ok(Err(format!(
                "Not started: {running} members you started are still running, and at most {} \
                 may run at once. End your turn; you will be told when they finish.",
                delegate.at_once
            )));
        }
        let step_run = self.store.get_step_run(&step.step_run_id).await?;
        let wr = self
            .store
            .get_workflow_run(&step_run.workflow_run_id)
            .await?;
        if let Some(limit) = budget_reached(&wr) {
            return Ok(Err(format!("Not started: Budget reached: {limit}.")));
        }
        if let Some(refusal) = self.member_refusal(&member.dot_id).await? {
            return Ok(Err(format!("Not started: {refusal}.")));
        }
        let Some(definition_step) = wr.definition.step(&step.step_id) else {
            return Err(Error::Other(format!(
                "step {} is not in the workflow",
                step.step_id
            )));
        };
        let section = self
            .member_section(&wr, definition_step, &member.dot_id, task)
            .await?;
        // A thread of its own (no parent, so its own root), working for the step run: it counts
        // against the budget as the step's own runs do, and starts where the step itself did.
        let mut new = NewRun::new(&member.dot_id, TriggerKind::Workflow);
        new.workflow_step_run_id = Some(step.step_run_id.clone());
        new.payload = Some(json!({
            "workflow": {
                "workflow_run_id": wr.id,
                "step_id": step.step_id,
                "start": start_branch(&wr, &step.step_id),
                "section": section,
            }
        }));
        let run = self.store.add_step_member(&new, task).await?;
        self.runner.enqueued(&run);
        self.updated(&self.store.get_workflow_run(&wr.id).await?);
        Ok(Ok(format!("Started \"{}\".", member.name)))
    }

```

### Task 6: Send-backs resume the earlier step where it left off, within the cap

The person sends a check back with a note (`send_back_check`), and an agent's recorded `send_back` is performed when its step succeeds (`move_on`). The target gets a new attempt, and its last run is continued in its own session with the note; the steps after it are continued in turn with the new hand-off. A run whose session is gone starts fresh and its step run's `notice` says so. Past `max_send_backs` the send-back is held and the run waits until the person allows one more (`allow_send_back`). A delegate's `total` now counts over every attempt.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/dots-core/src/workflows/send_back.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dots-core/migrations/0027_teams_b.sql`, `crates/dots-core/src/{model.rs,workflows.rs,workflows/delegate.rs,workflows/jobs.rs,workflows/tools.rs,store/step_members.rs,store/workflow_runs.rs}`
- Test: `crates/dots-core/tests/{workflows_test,workflow_tools_test}.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4's `StepRun.send_back` and `call_send_back`; Task 5's `own_runs`, `step_members`; Task 3's hand-off job; Task 2's `ran_before`, `handoffs_before`, `start_branch`.
- Produces: `model::{SendBack { from, to, note, by: SentBy, held: bool }, SentBy { Person, Agent } (lowercase), WorkflowRun.send_back: Option<SendBack>, WorkflowRunChange.send_back: Option<Option<SendBack>>}`; `Store::step_members_started(&self, step_run_id: &str) -> Result<u32>`; `Workflows::{send_back_check(&self, id: &str, note: &str) -> Result<WorkflowRun> (AnswerApprovals), allow_send_back(&self, id: &str) -> Result<WorkflowRun> (RunDots)}`; check decision `{"decision": "sent_back", "note": …}`; crate-private in `workflows/send_back.rs`: `check_target(wr, check: &Step) -> Option<&Step>`, `move_on(&self, wr: &WorkflowRun, step: &Step) -> Result<()>`, `queue_first(&self, wr: &WorkflowRun, step: &Step, step_run: &StepRun) -> Result<()>`, `Again { start: Option<String>, message: String }`; in `workflows.rs`: `start_agent(wr, step, step_run)`, `queue_agent_run(…, again: Option<&send_back::Again>)`, `section(…, sent: Option<&str>)`.

```rust
/// An agent step of `member` that may send the work back to the steps `to`.
fn sending_back(id: &str, name: &str, member: &Dot, job: &str, to: &[&str]) -> Step {
    let mut step = agent(id, name, member, job);
    if let StepKind::Agent {
        can_send_back_to, ..
    } = &mut step.kind
    {
        *can_send_back_to = to.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect();
    }
    step
}

/// The step runs of the workflow run as (step id, attempt, status), in the order made.
fn attempts(wr: &WorkflowRun) -> Vec<(&str, u32, StepRunStatus)> {
    wr.steps
        .iter()
        .map(|s| (s.step_id.as_str(), s.attempt, s.status))
        .collect()
}

/// The newest step run of `step`.
fn newest<'a>(wr: &'a WorkflowRun, step: &str) -> &'a StepRun {
    wr.steps.iter().rev().find(|s| s.step_id == step).unwrap()
}

/// Waiting on the check `step`, which has had `n` step runs.
fn at(step: &'static str, n: usize) -> impl Fn(&WorkflowRun) -> bool {
    move |wr| {
        waits_on(WaitingOn::Check)(wr)
            && wr.current_step.as_deref() == Some(step)
            && wr.steps.iter().filter(|s| s.step_id == step).count() == n
    }
}

/// Waiting on the check `check`, which has had `n` step runs.
fn at_check(n: usize) -> impl Fn(&WorkflowRun) -> bool {
    at("check", n)
}

impl World {
    /// The runs of the step run `step_run`, oldest first.
    async fn runs_of(&self, step_run: &str) -> Vec<Run> {
        self.store.runs_of_step_run(step_run).await.unwrap()
    }

    /// As `until`, with a pass of `workflows` before each look, for a test whose loop is
    /// stopped.
    async fn until_passing(
        &self,
        workflows: &Workflows,
        id: &str,
        done: impl Fn(&WorkflowRun) -> bool,
    ) -> WorkflowRun {
        let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(60);
        loop {
            workflows.pass().await.unwrap();
            let wr = self.store.get_workflow_run(id).await.unwrap();
            if done(&wr) {
                return wr;
            }
            assert!(
                std::time::Instant::now() < deadline,
                "workflow run {id} did not get there: {wr:#?}"
            );
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(30)).await;
        }
    }
}

/// The person's send-back of `note` from the check `check` to the step `to`.
fn by_person(check: &str, to: &str, note: &str, held: bool) -> Option<SendBack> {
    Some(SendBack {
        from: check.into(),
        to: to.into(),
        note: note.into(),
        by: SentBy::Person,
        held,
    })
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_person_sends_back_from_a_check_and_the_step_goes_on_in_its_own_session() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.helper.spec.name,
        vec![Act::Finish("Task: make the icon visible.")],
    );
    w.engine.set(
        &w.dev.spec.name,
        vec![
            Act::Write("icon.txt", "Made the icon visible."),
            Act::Write("dark.txt", "Used the light set."),
        ],
    );
    let flow = w.standard().await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, at_check(1)).await;
    let first = w.runs_of(&newest(&wr, "dev").id).await.remove(0);
    let branch = first.branch.clone().unwrap();

    let sent = w
        .workflows
        .send_back_check(&wr.id, "  Use the light icon set.  ")
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(sent.send_backs, 1);
    assert_eq!(
        sent.send_back,
        by_person("check", "dev", "Use the light icon set.", false)
    );
    let decided = &sent.steps[2];
    assert_eq!(decided.status, StepRunStatus::Succeeded);
    assert_eq!(
        decided.decision,
        Some(json!({"decision": "sent_back", "note": "Use the light icon set."}))
    );

    // The check waits again, on the dev's new work.
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, at_check(2)).await;
    assert_eq!(
        attempts(&wr),
        [
            ("helper", 1, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("dev", 1, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("check", 1, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("dev", 2, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("check", 2, StepRunStatus::Waiting),
        ]
    );
    // The dev's run is continued for the new step run: the same session, worktree and branch.
    let again = newest(&wr, "dev");
    let runs = w.runs_of(&again.id).await;
    assert_eq!(runs.len(), 1);
    let run = &runs[0];
    assert_eq!(run.parent_run_id.as_deref(), Some(first.id.as_str()));
    assert_eq!(run.root_run_id, first.root_run_id);
    assert!(run.session_id.is_some());
    assert_eq!(run.session_id, first.session_id);
    assert_eq!(run.workspace_path, first.workspace_path);
    assert_eq!(run.branch.as_deref(), Some(branch.as_str()));
    assert_eq!(run.workflow_step_run_id.as_deref(), Some(again.id.as_str()));
    assert_eq!(
        w.engine.prompts_of("dev")[1],
        "The person sent this back: Use the light icon set."
    );
    // Its new commit is on that branch, over the first one, and the hand-off names it.
    let handoff = again.handoff.clone().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(handoff.summary, "Used the light set.");
    assert_eq!(handoff.branches.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(handoff.branches[0].branch, branch);
    assert_eq!(
        git_out(&w.repo, &["show", &format!("{branch}:dark.txt")]),
        "Used the light set."
    );
    assert_eq!(
        git_out(&w.repo, &["show", &format!("{branch}:icon.txt")]),
        "Made the icon visible."
    );
    // The step before the target does not run again.
    assert_eq!(w.engine.prompts_of("helper").len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(wr.spent_runs, 3);

    let done = w.workflows.approve_check(&wr.id, None).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(done.status, WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded);
    assert_eq!(
        done.steps[4].handoff.as_ref().unwrap().summary,
        "Used the light set."
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn an_agents_send_back_goes_when_its_step_ends_and_the_steps_between_run_again() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.dev.spec.name,
        vec![
            Act::Write("icon.txt", "Made the icon visible."),
            Act::Write("dark.txt", "Used the light set."),
        ],
    );
    w.engine.set(
        &w.helper.spec.name,
        vec![
            Act::Write("notes.txt", "Notes one."),
            // The review makes no change: its branch is gone when it runs again.
            Act::SendBack(
                "build",
                "Use the light set.",
                Box::new(Act::Finish("Sent it back.")),
            ),
            Act::Write("notes.txt", "Notes two."),
            Act::Write("review.txt", "Looks right now."),
        ],
    );
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Fix",
            vec![
                agent("build", "Build", &w.dev, "Fix the bug."),
                agent("notes", "Notes", &w.helper, "Write release notes."),
                sending_back("review", "Review", &w.helper, "Review it.", &["build"]),
                check("check"),
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, at_check(1)).await;
    assert_eq!(
        attempts(&wr),
        [
            ("build", 1, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("notes", 1, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("review", 1, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("build", 2, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("notes", 2, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("review", 2, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("check", 1, StepRunStatus::Waiting),
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        w.engine.answers(),
        [Ok(
            "Sent back to \"Build\". End your turn now: the work goes back when you finish."
                .to_string()
        )]
    );
    assert_eq!(wr.send_backs, 1);
    assert_eq!(
        wr.send_back,
        Some(SendBack {
            from: "review".into(),
            to: "build".into(),
            note: "Use the light set.".into(),
            by: SentBy::Agent,
            held: false,
        })
    );
    assert_eq!(
        wr.steps[2].send_back,
        Some(json!({"to": "build", "note": "Use the light set."}))
    );
    assert_eq!(newest(&wr, "review").send_back, None);
    assert_eq!(
        w.engine.prompts_of("dev")[1],
        "The step \"Review\" sent this back: Use the light set."
    );

    // The notes step is continued with the build's new hand-off. Its branch still has its
    // notes, so it is told to merge the new work in.
    let build = newest(&wr, "build").handoff.clone().unwrap().branches[0]
        .branch
        .clone();
    let notes_first = w.runs_of(&wr.steps[1].id).await.remove(0);
    let notes_again = w.runs_of(&newest(&wr, "notes").id).await.remove(0);
    assert_eq!(
        notes_again.parent_run_id.as_deref(),
        Some(notes_first.id.as_str())
    );
    assert_eq!(notes_again.session_id, notes_first.session_id);
    let helper = w.engine.prompts_of("helper");
    assert_eq!(helper.len(), 4);
    assert_eq!(
        helper[2],
        format!(
            "Work was sent back to \"Build\" and has run again. Its new hand-off:\n\n\
             Used the light set.\n\nBranches: {build}\n\nYour branch still has your earlier \
             commits: merge \"{build}\" into it before you go on.\n\nUpdate your work to match, \
             then end with a new summary."
        )
    );

    // The review is continued with the notes' new hand-off. Its branch was gone, so its
    // worktree is made again on that branch from the notes' new tip.
    let notes = newest(&wr, "notes").handoff.clone().unwrap().branches[0]
        .branch
        .clone();
    assert_eq!(
        helper[3],
        format!(
            "Work was sent back to \"Build\" and has run again. Its new hand-off:\n\n\
             Notes two.\n\nBranches: {notes}\n\nUpdate your work to match, then end with a new \
             summary."
        )
    );
    let review_first = w.runs_of(&wr.steps[2].id).await.remove(0);
    let review_again = w.runs_of(&newest(&wr, "review").id).await.remove(0);
    assert_eq!(
        review_again.parent_run_id.as_deref(),
        Some(review_first.id.as_str())
    );
    assert_eq!(review_again.session_id, review_first.session_id);
    assert_eq!(review_again.branch, review_first.branch);
    let tip = git_out(&w.repo, &["rev-parse", &format!("refs/heads/{notes}")]);
    assert_eq!(review_again.base_commit.as_deref(), Some(tip.as_str()));
    assert_ne!(review_again.base_commit, review_first.base_commit);
    let review = review_again.branch.clone().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        git_out(&w.repo, &["show", &format!("{review}:notes.txt")]),
        "Notes two."
    );
    assert_eq!(
        git_out(&w.repo, &["show", &format!("{review}:review.txt")]),
        "Looks right now."
    );
    // The check shows the review's new work.
    assert_eq!(
        newest(&wr, "review").handoff.as_ref().unwrap().branches[0].branch,
        review
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn past_the_cap_a_send_back_waits_until_the_person_allows_one_more() {
    let budget = Budget {
        max_runs: 50,
        ..Budget::default()
    };
    let w = world_with(Arc::new(SystemClock), budget).await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.dev.spec.name,
        vec![Act::Write("icon.txt", "Made the icon visible.")],
    );
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Fix",
            vec![
                agent("dev", "Fix it", &w.dev, "Fix the bug."),
                check("check"),
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    for (n, note) in ["One.", "Two.", "Three."].into_iter().enumerate() {
        w.until(&wr.id, at_check(n + 1)).await;
        let sent = w.workflows.send_back_check(&wr.id, note).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(sent.send_backs, n as u32 + 1);
    }
    w.until(&wr.id, at_check(4)).await;

    // The fourth is held: nothing goes back, and a pass leaves it waiting.
    let held = w.workflows.send_back_check(&wr.id, "Four.").await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(held.status, WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting);
    assert_eq!(held.waiting_on, Some(WaitingOn::SendBacks));
    assert_eq!(
        held.error.as_deref(),
        Some("Send-back limit reached: 3 of 3")
    );
    assert_eq!(held.send_backs, 3);
    assert_eq!(held.send_back, by_person("check", "dev", "Four.", true));
    assert_eq!(held.current_step.as_deref(), Some("check"));
    w.workflows.pass().await.unwrap();
    let still = w.store.get_workflow_run(&wr.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(still.waiting_on, Some(WaitingOn::SendBacks));
    assert_eq!(still.steps.iter().filter(|s| s.step_id == "dev").count(), 4);
    assert_eq!(w.engine.prompts_of("dev").len(), 4);
    assert!(matches!(
        w.workflows.approve_check(&wr.id, None).await,
        Err(Error::Conflict(_))
    ));

    let allowed = w.workflows.allow_send_back(&wr.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(allowed.budget.max_send_backs, 4);
    assert_eq!(allowed.send_backs, 4);
    assert_eq!(allowed.status, WorkflowRunStatus::Running);
    assert_eq!(allowed.error, None);
    assert_eq!(allowed.send_back, by_person("check", "dev", "Four.", false));
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, at_check(5)).await;
    let dev = w.engine.prompts_of("dev");
    assert_eq!(dev.len(), 5);
    assert_eq!(dev[4], "The person sent this back: Four.");
    match w.workflows.allow_send_back(&wr.id).await {
        Err(Error::Conflict(m)) => assert_eq!(
            m,
            format!(
                "workflow run {} is not waiting at its send-back limit",
                wr.id
            )
        ),
        other => panic!("{other:?}"),
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_send_back_past_the_runs_limit_waits_on_budget_then_goes_on() {
    let budget = Budget {
        max_runs: 1,
        ..Budget::default()
    };
    let w = world_with(Arc::new(SystemClock), budget).await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.dev.spec.name,
        vec![
            Act::Write("icon.txt", "Made the icon visible."),
            Act::Write("dark.txt", "Used the light set."),
        ],
    );
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Fix",
            vec![
                agent("dev", "Fix it", &w.dev, "Fix the bug."),
                check("check"),
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    w.until(&wr.id, at_check(1)).await;
    let sent = w
        .workflows
        .send_back_check(&wr.id, "Use the light set.")
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(sent.waiting_on, Some(WaitingOn::Budget));
    assert_eq!(sent.error.as_deref(), Some("Budget reached: runs, 1 of 1"));
    assert_eq!(sent.send_backs, 1);
    assert_eq!(sent.current_step.as_deref(), Some("dev"));
    let again = newest(&sent, "dev");
    assert_eq!((again.attempt, again.status), (2, StepRunStatus::Running));
    assert!(w.runs_of(&again.id).await.is_empty());

    w.workflows.add_budget(&wr.id).await.unwrap();
    w.until(&wr.id, at_check(2)).await;
    let dev = w.engine.prompts_of("dev");
    assert_eq!(dev.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(dev[1], "The person sent this back: Use the light set.");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_step_whose_session_cannot_be_resumed_starts_again_and_says_so() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.helper.spec.name,
        vec![
            Act::NoSession(None, "Task: make the icon visible."),
            Act::Finish("Task: use the light set."),
        ],
    );
    w.engine.set(
        &w.dev.spec.name,
        vec![
            Act::NoSession(Some("icon.txt"), "Made the icon visible."),
            Act::Write("dark.txt", "Used the light set."),
        ],
    );
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Fix",
            vec![
                agent("helper", "Write the task", &w.helper, "Write the task."),
                Step {
                    id: "task".into(),
                    name: "Task check".into(),
                    kind: StepKind::Check { send_back_to: None },
                },
                agent("dev", "Fix it", &w.dev, "Fix the bug."),
                check("check"),
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;

    // The helper made no change: it starts again as it started the first time.
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, at("task", 1)).await;
    w.workflows
        .send_back_check(&wr.id, "Say which icon.")
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, at("task", 2)).await;
    let helper = newest(&wr, "helper");
    assert_eq!(
        helper.notice.as_deref(),
        Some("Started again from the start: the earlier session could not be resumed.")
    );
    let fresh = w.runs_of(&helper.id).await.remove(0);
    assert_eq!(fresh.trigger, TriggerKind::Workflow);
    assert_eq!(fresh.parent_run_id, None);
    assert_eq!(
        fresh.payload.as_ref().unwrap()["workflow"]["start"],
        json!(null)
    );
    let prompt = &w.engine.prompts_of("helper")[1];
    for part in [
        "## Workflow",
        "### Your job in this step\n\nWrite the task.",
        "### Sent back\n\nThe person sent this back: Say which icon.\n\nWhen you are done",
    ] {
        assert!(prompt.contains(part), "missing {part:?} in:\n{prompt}");
    }
    assert_eq!(wr.steps[0].notice, None);

    // The dev made a change: it starts again from its old branch.
    w.workflows.approve_check(&wr.id, None).await.unwrap();
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, at_check(1)).await;
    let old = w.runs_of(&newest(&wr, "dev").id).await.remove(0);
    let old_branch = old.branch.clone().unwrap();
    w.workflows
        .send_back_check(&wr.id, "Use the light set.")
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, at_check(2)).await;
    let dev = newest(&wr, "dev");
    assert_eq!(
        dev.notice.clone(),
        Some(format!(
            "Started again from \"{old_branch}\": the earlier session could not be resumed."
        ))
    );
    let fresh = w.runs_of(&dev.id).await.remove(0);
    assert_eq!(fresh.parent_run_id, None);
    assert_eq!(
        fresh.payload.as_ref().unwrap()["workflow"]["start"],
        json!(format!("refs/heads/{old_branch}"))
    );
    let prompt = &w.engine.prompts_of("dev")[1];
    assert!(
        prompt.contains("### Sent back\n\nThe person sent this back: Use the light set.\n\n"),
        "{prompt}"
    );
    let branch = &dev.handoff.as_ref().unwrap().branches[0].branch;
    assert_ne!(branch, &old_branch);
    assert_eq!(
        git_out(&w.repo, &["show", &format!("{branch}:icon.txt")]),
        "Made the icon visible."
    );
    assert_eq!(
        git_out(&w.repo, &["show", &format!("{branch}:dark.txt")]),
        "Used the light set."
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_send_back_to_a_delegate_step_continues_the_delegating_run() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.helper.spec.name,
        vec![
            Act::Start(
                vec![("dev", "Part one.")],
                Box::new(Act::Finish("Started.")),
            ),
            Act::Finish("Merged it."),
            Act::Finish("Took the note."),
        ],
    );
    w.engine.set(
        &w.dev.spec.name,
        vec![Act::Write("one.txt", "Part one done.")],
    );
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Fix",
            vec![
                delegating("split", "Split it", &w.helper, &[&w.dev], 3, 6),
                check("check"),
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, at_check(1)).await;
    let first = newest(&wr, "split");
    let members = w.store.step_members(&first.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(members.len(), 1);
    let lead = w
        .runs_of(&first.id)
        .await
        .into_iter()
        .rev()
        .find(|r| !members.iter().any(|m| m.run_id == r.root_run_id))
        .unwrap();
    w.workflows
        .send_back_check(&wr.id, "Split it finer.")
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, at_check(2)).await;
    let again = newest(&wr, "split");
    let runs = w.runs_of(&again.id).await;
    assert_eq!(runs.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(runs[0].parent_run_id.as_deref(), Some(lead.id.as_str()));
    assert_eq!(runs[0].root_run_id, lead.root_run_id);
    assert_eq!(runs[0].session_id, lead.session_id);
    assert_eq!(
        w.engine.prompts_of("helper")[2],
        "The person sent this back: Split it finer."
    );
    // It started no member this time; the member did not run again.
    assert!(w.store.step_members(&again.id).await.unwrap().is_empty());
    assert_eq!(w.engine.prompts_of("dev").len(), 1);
    let handoff = again.handoff.clone().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(handoff.summary, "Took the note.");
    assert!(handoff.members.is_empty());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn send_back_check_refuses_a_bad_note_a_check_with_no_agent_before_it_and_no_check() {
    let w = world().await;
    let flow = w.workflow("Look", vec![check("check")]).await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, at_check(1)).await;
    let long = "x".repeat(8_001);
    for note in ["", "   ", long.as_str()] {
        match w.workflows.send_back_check(&wr.id, note).await {
            Err(Error::Invalid(m)) => assert_eq!(m, "the note must be 1 to 8,000 characters"),
            other => panic!("{other:?}"),
        }
    }
    match w.workflows.send_back_check(&wr.id, "Redo it.").await {
        Err(Error::Invalid(m)) => assert_eq!(
            m,
            "the check \"Your check\" has no agent step before it to send the work back to"
        ),
        other => panic!("{other:?}"),
    }
    // Nothing was written.
    let still = w.store.get_workflow_run(&wr.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(attempts(&still), [("check", 1, StepRunStatus::Waiting)]);
    assert_eq!(still.waiting_on, Some(WaitingOn::Check));
    assert_eq!((still.send_backs, still.send_back.clone()), (0, None));

    w.workflows.approve_check(&wr.id, None).await.unwrap();
    match w.workflows.send_back_check(&wr.id, "Redo it.").await {
        Err(Error::Conflict(m)) => assert_eq!(
            m,
            format!("workflow run {} is not waiting for a check", wr.id)
        ),
        other => panic!("{other:?}"),
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_send_back_cut_off_by_a_restart_is_finished_by_the_next_pass_once() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.dev.spec.name,
        vec![
            Act::Write("icon.txt", "Made the icon visible."),
            Act::Write("light.txt", "Used the light set."),
            Act::Write("dark.txt", "Used the dark set."),
        ],
    );
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Fix",
            vec![
                agent("dev", "Fix it", &w.dev, "Fix the bug."),
                check("check"),
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, at_check(1)).await;
    // Dots stops, with the loop, while the person's send-back is half made.
    w.stop_loop.cancel();
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
    let restarted = || {
        Workflows::new(
            w.store.clone(),
            new_bus(),
            w.runner.clone(),
            Arc::new(SystemClock),
        )
    };
    let decide = |note: &str| StepRunChange {
        status: Some(StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
        decision: Some(Some(json!({"decision": "sent_back", "note": note}))),
        ..Default::default()
    };

    // Cut off after the check was decided: nothing went back yet. The next passes send it
    // back once.
    w.store
        .set_step_run(&newest(&wr, "check").id, &decide("Use the light set."))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    w.store
        .set_workflow_run(
            &wr.id,
            &WorkflowRunChange {
                status: Some(WorkflowRunStatus::Running),
                waiting_on: Some(None),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        )
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let first = restarted();
    first.pass().await.unwrap();
    first.pass().await.unwrap();
    let moved = w.store.get_workflow_run(&wr.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(moved.send_backs, 1);
    assert_eq!(moved.current_step.as_deref(), Some("dev"));
    let dev2 = newest(&moved, "dev").clone();
    assert_eq!(dev2.attempt, 2);
    assert_eq!(w.runs_of(&dev2.id).await.len(), 1);
    let wr = w.until_passing(&first, &wr.id, at_check(2)).await;
    assert_eq!(
        w.engine.prompts_of("dev")[1],
        "The person sent this back: Use the light set."
    );

    // Cut off after the dev's new step run was made and the run moved to it, before its run
    // was queued: the next passes queue the continuation once.
    w.store
        .set_step_run(&newest(&wr, "check").id, &decide("Use the dark set."))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let dev3 = w
        .store
        .create_step_run(&wr.id, "dev", 3, StepRunStatus::Running)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    w.store
        .set_workflow_run(
            &wr.id,
            &WorkflowRunChange {
                status: Some(WorkflowRunStatus::Running),
                waiting_on: Some(None),
                current_step: Some(Some("dev".into())),
                send_backs: Some(2),
                send_back: Some(by_person("check", "dev", "Use the dark set.", false)),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        )
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let second = restarted();
    second.pass().await.unwrap();
    second.pass().await.unwrap();
    let runs = w.runs_of(&dev3.id).await;
    assert_eq!(runs.len(), 1);
    let dev2_run = w.runs_of(&dev2.id).await.remove(0);
    assert_eq!(runs[0].parent_run_id.as_deref(), Some(dev2_run.id.as_str()));
    let wr = w.until_passing(&second, &wr.id, at_check(3)).await;
    assert_eq!(wr.send_backs, 2);
    assert_eq!(
        w.engine.prompts_of("dev")[2],
        "The person sent this back: Use the dark set."
    );
    assert_eq!(
        attempts(&wr),
        [
            ("dev", 1, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("check", 1, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("dev", 2, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("check", 2, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("dev", 3, StepRunStatus::Succeeded),
            ("check", 3, StepRunStatus::Waiting),
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(w.engine.prompts_of("dev").len(), 3);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_delegate_step_sent_back_counts_its_total_over_every_attempt() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.engine.set(
        &w.helper.spec.name,
        vec![
            Act::Start(
                vec![("dev", "Part one.")],
                Box::new(Act::Finish("Started.")),
            ),
            Act::Finish("Merged it."),
            Act::Start(
                vec![("dev", "Part two."), ("dev", "Part three.")],
                Box::new(Act::Finish("Started again.")),
            ),
            Act::Finish("Done again."),
        ],
    );
    w.engine.set(
        &w.dev.spec.name,
        vec![Act::Finish("Part one done."), Act::Finish("Part two done.")],
    );
    let flow = w
        .workflow(
            "Fix",
            vec![
                delegating("split", "Split it", &w.helper, &[&w.dev], 2, 2),
                check("check"),
            ],
        )
        .await;
    let wr = w.start(&flow).await;
    w.until(&wr.id, at_check(1)).await;
    let helper = w.engine.prompts_of("helper");
    assert!(
        helper[1].ends_with(
            "You may start 1 more with `mcp__dots__start_member`, or end with your summary of \
             the step."
        ),
        "{}",
        helper[1]
    );
    w.workflows
        .send_back_check(&wr.id, "Split it finer.")
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let wr = w.until(&wr.id, at_check(2)).await;
    // The member started in the first attempt counts: the second may start one more, and the
    // third is refused.
    assert_eq!(
        w.engine.answers(),
        [
            Ok("Started \"dev\".".to_string()),
            Ok("Started \"dev\".".to_string()),
            Err("Not started: you have started 2 members, the most this step allows.".into()),
        ]
    );
    let again = newest(&wr, "split");
    assert_eq!(w.store.step_members(&again.id).await.unwrap().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(w.store.step_members_started(&again.id).await.unwrap(), 2);
    let helper = w.engine.prompts_of("helper");
    assert_eq!(helper.len(), 4);
    assert_eq!(helper[2], "The person sent this back: Split it finer.");
    assert!(
        helper[3].contains("#### dev: Part two.")
            && helper[3]
                .ends_with("You may start no more members: end with your summary of the step."),
        "{}",
        helper[3]
    );
    assert_eq!(again.handoff.as_ref().unwrap().summary, "Done again.");
}
```

```rust
/// Work sent back to an earlier agent step (teams spec sections 5.4 and 9): by the person from
/// a check, or by an agent step that asked for it with `send_back`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SendBack {
    /// The id of the step that sent it back.
    pub from: String,
    /// The id of the agent step it goes back to.
    pub to: String,
    pub note: String,
    pub by: SentBy,
    /// Held at the cap of send-backs: not performed until the person allows it.
    pub held: bool,
}

/// Who sent work back.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum SentBy {
    Person,
    Agent,
}
```
